# Lido Lend Markets — Test Plan

One line per check: what it verifies, then the tests that verify it. `[x]` has a test, `[ ]` has none, `[-]` cannot happen. A test name without a path is in the first file of its contract's `Tests:` line. Every line reads: what is checked — the tests or files that check it. Each test and each contract member links to the line that declares it; `make test-plan` relinks the plan after an edit, and `make test-plan-check` fails while a link is stale.

Requirements come from [docs/specs/](docs/specs/README.md) and [docs/integration/bundler.md](docs/integration/bundler.md). The forked core and the IRMs have their own plans: `lib/lend/TEST_PLAN.md`, `lib/lend-irm/TEST_PLAN.md`.

## How to run
- `make build && make test-local` — unit, scenario, invariant and 30-day simulation suites; the build compiles the Bundler3 artifact the bundler suites load
- `make test-simulation` — the 365-day simulations
- `make test-fork RPC_URL=<rpc>` — fresh mainnet fixture inside Forge; `RUN=<run>` prepares missing/inert recorded markets; add `PREPARE=false` to check existing state without preparation
- `make test-deployment RPC_URL=<rpc>` — deployment and market preparation scenarios inside a private Forge fork
- `make acceptance-run RUN=<run> MARKET=<market>`, `make acceptance-run-multi RUN=<run> MARKET_A=<a> MARKET_B=<b>` — acceptance of a deploy run
- `forge coverage --report summary --no-match-coverage "test|mocks" --no-match-path 'test/{fork,deployment}/**'` — coverage of `src/`
- CI, all dispatched by hand: `ci.yml` (local and fork jobs), `coverage.yml`, `fuzz.yml` (fuzz at 10 000 runs, invariants at 4096×64, 365-day simulations), `pending-action.yml` (a pending multisig action on a fork, checked with state-mate)

### `PausableUntil` — [`src/utils/PausableUntil.sol`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol) (`PausableUntilTest` harness and controller suite), [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol), [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol)

1. [`_pauseFor(uint256)`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol#L87) (via controller [`pauseFor`](src/controllers/LidoLendMarketController.sol#L361) and harness)
   - [x] positive: finite pause sets the resume timestamp to now + duration — [`test_pauseFor_finite_resumeTimestamp`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L941)
   - [x] positive: `PAUSE_INFINITELY` parks the resume timestamp at the sentinel — [`test_pauseFor_indefinite`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L921); [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_InfinitePause_NeverExpiresAndNeedsExplicitResume`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L308)
   - [x] negative: zero duration reverts `ZeroPauseDuration` — [`testRevert_pauseFor_zeroDuration`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L315)
   - [x] negative: already paused reverts `ResumedExpected` — [`testRevert_pauseFor_whenAlreadyPaused`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L947)
   - [x] boundaries: reads resumed exactly at now + duration — [`test_pause_autoResumesAfterDuration`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L308)
   - [x] boundaries: a finite duration that overflows `block.timestamp + duration` reverts — [`testRevert_pauseFor_durationOverflows`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2258)
2. [`_pauseUntil(uint256)`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol#L100) (harness only)
   - [x] positive: paused through the deadline, resumed the second after — [`test_pauseUntil_futureTimestamp`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2211)
   - [x] positive: `PAUSE_INFINITELY` parks the resume timestamp at the sentinel — [`test_pauseUntil_infinite`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2225)
   - [x] negative: a past target reverts `PauseUntilMustBeInFuture` — [`testRevert_pauseUntil_past`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2233)
   - [x] negative: already paused reverts `ResumedExpected` — [`testRevert_pauseUntil_whenAlreadyPaused`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2239)
   - [x] boundaries: a target equal to now is accepted (paused for that second) — [`test_pauseUntil_now_pausesForThatSecond`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2246)
3. [`_resume()`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol#L79)
   - [x] positive: lifts the pause and moves the resume timestamp to now — [`test_resume_early`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L336), [`test_pauseFor_indefinite`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L921)
   - [x] negative: not paused reverts `PausedExpected` — [`testRevert_resume_whenNotPaused`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L321); [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`testRevert_ResumeWhileNotPaused_Rejected`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L332)
   - [x] negative: after a finite pause expires, resume reverts `PausedExpected` — [`testRevert_resume_afterFinitePauseExpired`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L327)
4. [`_checkPaused()`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol#L69), [`_checkResumed()`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol#L74)
   - [x] negative: `_checkPaused` reverts `PausedExpected` when resumed — [`testRevert_checkPaused_whenResumed`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2267)
   - [x] positive: `_checkPaused` passes while paused — [`test_checkPaused_whenPaused`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2273)
   - [x] negative: `_checkResumed` makes every controller hook revert while paused — [`test_pause_haltsActions`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L264); [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`testRevert_Paused_SupplyCollateralRejected`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L129), [`testRevert_Paused_WithdrawCollateralRejected`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L141), [`testRevert_Paused_RepayRejected`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L117)
5. views: [`isPaused`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol#L55), [`getResumeSinceTimestamp`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol#L60)
   - [x] positive: a fresh contract reads not paused — [`test_views_defaults`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L1389)
   - [x] positive: a finite pause expires without intervention — [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_FinitePause_ExpiresWithoutIntervention`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L297)
   - [x] positive: resume timestamp reported for finite and infinite pauses — [`test_pauseFor_finite_resumeTimestamp`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L941), [`test_pauseUntil_infinite`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2225)

#### Events
1. [`Paused(uint256)`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol#L33)
   - [x] emitted with the remaining seconds for a finite pause (fork only) — [`test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_resealPauseFor_thenResume`](test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L97)
   - [x] emitted with `PAUSE_INFINITELY` for an indefinite pause — [`test_pauseFor_indefinite_emitsPausedInfinitely`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L933)
2. [`Resumed()`](src/utils/PausableUntil.sol#L35)
   - [x] emitted on resume (fork only) — [`test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_resealPauseFor_thenResume`](test/fork/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L97)

### `CollateralActivationLib` — [`src/libs/CollateralActivationLib.sol`](src/libs/CollateralActivationLib.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol), [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol), [`test/scenario/Activation.t.sol`](test/scenario/Activation.t.sol), [`test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol), [`test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol), [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol), [`test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol), [`test/invariant/GuardianActivation.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianActivation.invariant.t.sol), [`test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol), [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol)

1. [`setConfig(State,CollateralActivationConfig)`](src/libs/CollateralActivationLib.sol#L179)
   - [x] positive: in-bound delay floor and cap are stored — [`test_setMinActivationDelay_updatesFloor`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L395), [`test_setMaxActivationDelay_updatesCap`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L402)
   - [x] negative: zero delay floor reverts `ZeroMinActivationDelay` — [`testRevert_setMinActivationDelay_zero`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L409)
   - [x] negative: floor above cap reverts `InvalidActivationDelays` — [`testRevert_setMinActivationDelay_aboveCap`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L415), [`testRevert_setMaxActivationDelay_belowFloor`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L421)
   - [x] negative: delay cap above 7 days reverts `MaxActivationDelayExceeded` — [`testRevert_setMaxActivationDelay_aboveBound`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L427)
   - [x] negative: lock above 7 days reverts `MaxActivatingLockDurationExceeded` — [`testRevert_setConfig_activatingLockDurationAboveBound`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1121)
   - [x] negative: nonzero quorum delay equal to the cap reverts `InvalidMaxGuardiansQuorumDelay` — [`testRevert_setMaxGuardiansQuorumDelay_notAboveMaxActivationDelay`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L434)
   - [x] boundaries: quorum delay 7 days accepted, 7 days + 1 reverts — [`test_setMaxGuardiansQuorumDelay_atBound`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L440), [`testRevert_setMaxGuardiansQuorumDelay_aboveBound`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L446)
   - [x] negative: zero `minActivationAmount` or `maxDelayInflow` reverts — [`testRevert_setConfig_zeroMinActivationAmount`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1129), [`testRevert_setConfig_zeroMaxDelayInflow`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1157)
   - [x] boundaries: `minDepositAmount` equal to the threshold accepted, one above reverts — [`test_setConfig_minDepositAmountAtActivationAmount`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1144), [`testRevert_setConfig_minDepositAmountAboveActivationAmount`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1136)
   - [x] negative: strike or suspect surcharge above 7 days reverts — [`testRevert_setConfig_delayPerStrikeAboveBound`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1165), [`testRevert_setConfig_delayPerSuspectAboveBound`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1173)
   - [x] state effects: a cap lowered below the standing inflow clamps without underflow — [`test_capLoweredBelowBaseline_clampsWithoutUnderflow`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1090)
   - [x] boundaries: delay cap and lock duration exactly 7 days accepted — [`test_setConfig_delayCapAndLockAtSevenDays_accepted`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L452)
   - [x] boundaries: floor equal to cap accepted — [`test_setConfig_floorEqualToCap_accepted`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L463)
2. [`credit(State,address,uint256,bool)`](src/libs/CollateralActivationLib.sol#L212)
   - [x] positive: a threshold-crossing deposit enters activating and bumps inflow — [`test_flashDepositDustWithdraw_doesNotInflateThrottle`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L195), [`test_inflowAccumulator_capsAtMaxDelayInflow`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L176)
   - [x] positive: a sub-threshold deposit on empty dust parks as dust, no inflow — [`test_dustThenCrossThreshold_releasesDustIntoActivating`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L224), [`test_flashDepositDustWithdraw_doesNotInflateThrottle`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L195)
   - [x] positive: dust then a crossing deposit promote together into activating — [`test_dustThenCrossThreshold_releasesDustIntoActivating`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L224)
   - [x] negative: a sub-threshold deposit onto dust reverts `DustNotResolved` — [`testRevert_deposit_ontoDust_belowThreshold`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L245)
   - [x] boundaries: dust plus deposit exactly at the threshold promotes the whole lot — [`test_deposit_ontoDust_atThreshold_promotesWholeLot`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L256)
   - [x] positive: dust is allowed beside an existing activating lot — [`test_deposit_subThreshold_withActivatingLot_createsSingleDustLot`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L268)
   - [x] negative: below `minDepositAmount` reverts `DepositTooSmall` — [`testRevert_credit_belowMinDepositAmount`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L277)
   - [x] boundaries: deposit at `minDepositAmount` accepted, zero floor disables it — [`test_credit_atMinDepositAmount_accepted`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L287), [`test_credit_zeroMinDepositAmount_disablesFloor`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L299)
   - [x] boundaries: exact-threshold lot activates, one under stays dust — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_activationFloor_exactThresholdActivates`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L162)
   - [x] positive: instant deposit counts active, no timer, no inflow, no deposit floor — [`test_instantDeposit_activatesImmediatelyWithoutInflow`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L329), [`test_instantDeposit_bypassesMinDepositAmount`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L315)
   - [x] state effects: an instant deposit leaves existing dust untouched — [`test_instantDeposit_dustPlusDepositAtThreshold_activatesOnlyDeposit`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L340)
   - [x] state effects: inflow saturates at `maxDelayInflow` — [`test_inflowAccumulator_capsAtMaxDelayInflow`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L176); [`test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_inflowNeverExceedsCap`](test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol#L34)
   - [x] state effects: a top-up restarting the timer past suspect expiry sheds the surcharge — [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol): [`test_staleCount_topUpOntoAnUnmaturedLot_shedsTheSurcharge`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol#L282)
   - [x] state effects: a top-up with an earlier deadline keeps the existing timer — [`test_credit_topUpWithEarlierDeadline_keepsTimer`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L899)
3. [`debit(State,address,uint256,uint256)`](src/libs/CollateralActivationLib.sol#L255)
   - [x] negative: a draw short of the inactive balance reverts `InactiveCollateralNotCleared` — [`testRevert_withdraw_partialInactive`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L476), [`testRevert_withdraw_belowInactive_evenWithinActive`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L605)
   - [x] positive: a full inactive clear empties buckets, timer and same-tx inflow — [`test_withdraw_fullInactive_clearsBucketsTimerAndInflow`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L488)
   - [x] positive: no inactive balance means an unrestricted active draw — [`test_withdraw_activeOnly_isUnrestricted`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L512)
   - [x] positive: an amount above inactive clears it, the rest comes from active — [`test_withdraw_amountAboveInactive_clearsInactiveThenDrawsFromActive`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L524)
   - [x] negative: a locked lot with no active reverts `ActivatingCollateralLocked` — [`testRevert_withdraw_lockedActivating_blocksClearing`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L541)
   - [x] boundaries: under lock exactly active passes, one wei more reverts — [`testRevert_withdraw_lockedBeyondActive`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L580)
   - [x] state effects: under lock even an inactive-covering draw stays active-only — [`test_withdraw_activeWithinLock_ignoresLock`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L554), [`test_withdraw_lockedCoveringInactive_staysActiveOnly`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L622)
   - [x] boundaries: at exactly the lock deadline the lot clears — [`test_withdraw_afterLockExpiry_clearsInactive`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L653)
   - [x] positive: dust-only position and zero lock duration clear at once — [`test_withdraw_dustOnly_unaffectedByLockWindow`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L640), [`test_withdraw_zeroLockDuration_allowsImmediateSameTxClear`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L664)
   - [x] state effects: deposit-withdraw cycles leave no residual inflow — [`test_debit_repeatedFullClearCycles_noInflowAccumulation`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L699), [`testFuzz_debit_emptyPositionHasNoResidualInflow`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L718)
   - [x] state effects: reverses only this tx's own contribution — [`test_overWithdraw_reversesOnlyThisTxContribution`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1333), [`test_capFilling_tempBeforePermanent_withdrawKeepsInflow`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L961), [`test_multipleBorrowers_grossExceedsCap_reversalTracksRemainder`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L995)
   - [x] state effects: stored inflow stays min(cap, baseline + net) across mixed ops — [`test_mixedOps_maintainInvariant`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1017), [`testFuzz_invariant_cappedBaselinePlusNet`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L1069)
4. [`sync(State,bool,address,GuardiansOpinion)`](src/libs/CollateralActivationLib.sol#L294)
   - [x] positive: the lot matures once the quorum scans past the unlock — [`test_sync_gateHoldsUntilQuorumScansPastUnlock`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L357)
   - [x] negative: a quorum frontier behind the unlock holds the lot — [`test_sync_gateHoldsUntilQuorumScansPastUnlock`](test/unit/CollateralActivationLib.t.sol#L357)

   - [x] boundaries: matures with the frontier exactly at `unlockTime`, not one second before — [`test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol): [`test_QuorumOfHeartbeatsPastUnlock_MaturesLot`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol#L129), [`testRevert_QuorumHeartbeatsBeforeUnlock_StillBlocked`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol#L143)
5. [`seize(State,address,uint256,uint256)`](src/libs/CollateralActivationLib.sol#L322)

### `SettlementQueueLib` — [`src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol), [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol), [`test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol), [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol)

1. [`init(State)`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L80)
   - [x] positive: a fresh queue is empty with a zero root — [`test_peekRoot_empty_isZeroAddress`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L367)
2. [`sync(State,address,PositionView)`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L85)
   - [x] positive: enqueues by borrowShares / collateral, riskiest at the root — [`test_sync_enqueuesRemovesRekeys`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L210)
   - [x] state effects: zero collateral or zero borrow shares removes the borrower — [`test_sync_enqueuesRemovesRekeys`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L210), [`test_sync_zeroBorrowShares_removes`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L252)
   - [x] state effects: a re-key updates in place and can change the root — [`test_sync_rekeyChangesRoot`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L280)
   - [x] boundaries: keys above uint96 keep full precision and order — [`test_sync_ordersKeysAboveUint96Boundary`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L229), [`test_sync_sixDecimalCollateral_retainsOrdinaryLtvKey`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L242)
   - [x] positive: equal debt on half the collateral doubles the key — [`test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol): [`test_settlementQueue_rootIsTheRiskiest`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol#L506)
3. [`setSettlementConfig(State,SettlementConfig)`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L95)
   - [x] boundaries: both bps at 10_000 accepted — [`test_setSettlementConfig_bpsBoundaryAllowed`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L162)
   - [x] negative: `sliceFloorBps` or `ltvStepBps` above 10_000 reverts `SettlementConfigOutOfRange` — [`testRevert_setSettlementConfig_sliceFloorBpsOver`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L171), [`testRevert_setSettlementConfig_ltvStepBpsOver`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L179)
   - [x] state effects: every field stored and read back — [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol): [`test_setSettlementConfig_storesAndRoleGated`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L1589)
4. [`setSettlementLtv(State,address,uint256)`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L103)
   - [x] positive: stores the target, 0 clears it — [`test_setSettlementLtv_storesAndClears`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L187)
   - [x] negative: WAD reverts `SettlementLtvOutOfRange` — [`testRevert_setSettlementLtv_geWad`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L195)
   - [x] boundaries: WAD - 1 accepted — [`test_setSettlementLtv_justBelowWadAllowed`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L201)
5. [`allowedSliceAssets(State,MarketView,address,PositionView)`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L156)
   - [x] positive: personal slice is `sliceToLtv` to the borrower's target — [`test_allowedSlice_settlementLtvCut_appliesToNonRoot`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L472)
   - [x] positive: root slice takes the bps floor when it is largest — [`test_allowedSlice_root_getsFloor`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L455)
   - [x] positive: absolute floor wins and is returned below debt — [`test_allowedSlice_absoluteFloorWins_belowDebt`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L432)
   - [x] positive: the LTV-step term beats the floor — [`test_allowedSlice_ltvStepTerm_winsForRoot`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L512)
   - [x] boundaries: both quotes are capped at the debt — [`test_allowedSlice_cappedAtDebt`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L413), [`test_allowedSlices_nonRoot_exposesProspectiveQueueRootCut`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L444)
   - [x] boundaries: dust lift is strict, remaining equal to the threshold does not lift — [`test_allowedSlice_dustLift_boundary`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L527)
   - [x] state effects: dust lift applies to the queue-root quote only — [`test_allowedSlices_dustLift_appliesOnlyToQueueRoot`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L541)
   - [x] state effects: zero `fullCutBelowCollateral` never lifts — [`test_allowedSlice_fullCutZero_neverLifts`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L580)
   - [x] state effects: personal and root quotes are computed independently — [`test_allowedSlice_canExcludeRootPolicy`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L397)
   - [x] negative: zero debt returns (0, 0) — [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol): [`test_settlementFor_ineligibleVsEligible`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L1931)
   - [x] negative: zero collateral value (zero price) returns (0, 0) — [`test_allowedSlice_zeroPrice_returnsNoQuotes`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L485)
   - [-] negative: zero price skips the dust lift (cannot happen: `allowedSliceAssets` returns earlier on a zero collateral value)
6. [`settlementSlice(MarketView,PositionView,uint256,uint256)`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L185)
   - [x] boundaries: an ample budget leaves every field unchanged — [`test_settlementSlice_ampleBudget_matchesTheUnshrunkQuote`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L614)
   - [x] state effects: a short budget shrinks the cut, fields still span the position — [`test_settlementSlice_shortBudget_keepsFieldsConsistent`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L627)
   - [x] negative: a positive budget funding nothing reports no cut — [`test_settlementSlice_unaffordable_isUnavailable`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L642)
   - [x] negative: a cut seizing under one collateral unit is unavailable — [`test_settlementSlice_cutBelowSeizureFloor_isUnavailable`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L668), [`test_settlementSlice_budgetShrunkOntoFloor_isUnavailable`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L695); [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_seizureFloor_subUnitCutIsUnavailable`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L311)
   - [x] boundaries: seizure floor tracks price, one wei above parity fails — [`test_settlementSlice_seizureFloorTracksPrice`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L685)
   - [x] negative: zero price is unavailable — [`test_settlementSlice_zeroPrice_isUnavailable`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L709)
   - [x] state effects: a near-full asset cut may round up to a full close — [`test_personalQuote_nearFullAssetCutMayRoundToFullBorrowShares`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L554)
   - [x] positive: a quoted cut always seizes a unit and its burn fits the budget — [`testFuzz_settlementSlice_quotedCutSeizesCollateral`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L719), [`testFuzz_settlementSlice_burnFitsInBudget`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L752), [`testFuzz_quotedSupplyCost_fundsTheQuotedCut`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L775)
   - [x] negative: zero borrow shares return an all-zero slice — [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol): [`test_nextSettlementBorrower_emptyQueue`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L1894)
   - [x] negative: zero `sliceAssets` returns no cut — [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol): [`test_settlementFor_ineligibleVsEligible`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L1931)
7. [`settlementLtvSlice(State,MarketView,address,PositionView)`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L224)
   - [x] positive: a target below live LTV gives a positive slice — [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol): [`test_setSettlementLtv`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L1534)
   - [x] negative: an unset target returns 0 — [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol): [`test_settlementLtv_unsetReturnsZero`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketController.t.sol#L2102)
   - [x] negative: a target at or above live LTV returns 0 — [`test_settlementLtvSlice_targetAtOrAboveLiveLtv_isZero`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L498)
8. [`sliceToLtv(uint256,uint256,uint256)`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L246)
   - [x] positive: closed form brings post-cut LTV to the target — [`test_sliceToLtv_closedForm`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L312)
   - [x] boundaries: target 0 is the full debt, target at or above WAD is 0 — [`test_sliceToLtv_targetZero_isFullDebt`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L294), [`test_sliceToLtv_targetAtOrAboveWad_isZero`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L299)
   - [x] boundaries: at or below target is 0, including exact equality — [`test_sliceToLtv_atOrBelowTarget_isZero`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L305), [`test_sliceToLtv_boundaryEquality`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L329)
   - [x] state effects: LTV above 1 returns an unclamped slice above debt — [`test_sliceToLtv_collatBelowDebt_canExceedDebt_unclamped`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L322)
   - [x] positive: fuzzed inputs match the floored recompute, never overshoot, monotonic — [`testFuzz_sliceToLtv_matchesRecompute_andNoOvershoot`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L338), [`testFuzz_sliceToLtv_monotonicInTarget`](test/unit/SettlementQueueLib.t.sol#L354)
9. views: [`settlementLtvOf`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L114), [`size`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L119), [`peekRoot`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L124), [`settlementBorrowers`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L132)

### `MaxHeapLib` — [`src/libs/MaxHeapLib.sol`](src/libs/MaxHeapLib.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol)

1. [`init(Heap)`](src/libs/MaxHeapLib.sol#L49)
   - [x] positive: a fresh heap has size 0 and `peek` reverts — [`testRevert_peekOnEmpty`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L294)
2. [`upsert(Heap,address,uint256)`](src/libs/MaxHeapLib.sol#L82)
   - [x] positive: a new maximum sifts up to the root — [`test_upsertFreshInsert_siftsUp`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L228), [`test_rootIsMax`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L311)
   - [x] positive: an existing account is re-keyed, not duplicated — [`test_upsertExisting_rekeysNotDuplicates`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L101)
   - [x] state effects: re-key up promotes, re-key down demotes the root — [`test_rekeyUp_promotesToRoot`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L152), [`test_rekeyDown_demotesRoot`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L163)
   - [x] boundaries: re-keying to the same value is a no-op — [`test_rekeySameValue_isNoop`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L249)
   - [x] boundaries: key 0 is present; `type(uint256).max` is retained — [`test_keyZero_isPresentNotAbsent`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L337), [`test_retainsKeysAboveUint96`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L325)
   - [x] boundaries: keys around uint96 stay distinct and ordered — [`test_keysAroundUint96Boundary_remainDistinct`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L114), [`test_keysAboveUint96Boundary_orderRegardlessOfInsertion`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L126), [`test_maxSettlementRiskKey_retainsFullPrecision`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L144)
   - [x] state effects: tied keys keep the heap property — [`test_tiedKeys_heapHolds`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L219)
3. [`remove(Heap,address)`](src/libs/MaxHeapLib.sol#L102)
   - [x] positive: removing the root returns true and promotes the next maximum — [`test_removeRoot_promotesNextMax`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L176)
   - [x] negative: an absent account returns false, heap unchanged — [`test_removeAbsent_isNoop`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L189)
   - [x] state effects: a back-filled node sifts up when its parent is smaller — [`test_removeTriggersSiftUp_maintainsHeap`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L200)
   - [x] boundaries: removing the only element empties the heap — [`test_removeOnlyElement_emptiesAndPeekReverts`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L240)
   - [x] state effects: random upsert/remove sequences keep size and heap property — [`testFuzz_heapPropertyHoldsAcrossSequence`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L263), [`testFuzz_drainIsNonIncreasing`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L356)
4. views: [`peek`](src/libs/MaxHeapLib.sol#L63), [`size`](src/libs/MaxHeapLib.sol#L58), [`nodeAt`](src/libs/MaxHeapLib.sol#L70)
   - [x] negative: `peek` on an empty heap reverts `EmptyHeap` — [`testRevert_peekOnEmpty`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L294)
   - [x] positive: a singleton peeks its account and key — [`test_singleton`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L300)
   - [x] negative: `nodeAt` past the last node reverts `IndexOutOfBounds` — [`testRevert_nodeAtOutOfRange`](test/unit/MaxHeapLib.t.sol#L348)

#### Events
None.

   - [x] positive: pulls exactly `previewMint` assets — [`test_mintRedeem_roundTrip`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L206); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`testFuzz_previewMint_isExact`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L94)
   - [x] boundaries: minting exactly `maxMint` under a cap with accrued interest executes — [`test_maxMint_isExactMintBoundary_underCapWithInterest`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L932)
   - [x] negative: minting `maxMint + 1` under a cap reverts `ERC4626ExceededMaxMint` — [`test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol): [`test_GuardedRefusalsAreReachable`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L393)
5. `withdraw(uint256,address,address)`
   - [x] positive: burns exactly `previewWithdraw` shares for the assets — [`test_depositWithdraw_roundTrip`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L195); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`testFuzz_previewWithdraw_isExact`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L103)
   - [x] positive: on behalf spends allowance, pays the receiver, emits `Withdraw` — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`test_withdraw_onBehalf_spendsAllowance_paysReceiver`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L196)
   - [x] negative: on behalf without allowance reverts `ERC20InsufficientAllowance` — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`testRevert_withdraw_onBehalf_withoutAllowance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L217)
   - [x] negative: zero `maxWithdraw` rejects with `ERC4626ExceededMaxWithdraw` — [`test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol): [`test_GuardedRefusalsAreReachable`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L393); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`test_state_paused_disablesEverything`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L380)
   - [x] negative: an exit-book fill above the vault's free liquidity reverts and rolls back — [`test/scenario/ExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/ExitBook.t.sol): [`testRevert_insufficientSourceLiquidity_rollsBackRequestWithoutUsingExecutorAssets`](test/scenario/ExitBook.t.sol#L312)
   - [x] boundaries: under a liquidity cap `maxWithdraw` passes and `maxWithdraw + 1` reverts — [`test_withdraw_exactlyMaxWithdrawUnderLiquidityCap_passesOneMoreReverts`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L761)
   - [x] boundaries: a mint then a withdraw of `maxWithdraw` pays out no more loan tokens than the mint pulled — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`testFuzz_roundTrip_mintWithdraw_noFreeProfit`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L163)
   - [x] positive: still works while the market is frozen — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`test_state_frozen_lenderFlowsUnaffected`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L362)
   - [x] state effects: managed shares fall by exactly the core shares burned — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol): [`test_withdraw_partialThenFull_tracksManaged`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L369)
6. `redeem(uint256,address,address)`
   - [x] positive: pays exactly `previewRedeem` assets — [`test_mintRedeem_roundTrip`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L206); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`testFuzz_previewRedeem_isExact`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L115)
   - [x] boundaries: exactly `maxRedeem` passes, `maxRedeem + 1` reverts — [`test_maxRedeem_isHonouredConservativeBoundary_underLiquidityConstraint`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L251); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`test_maxWithdrawRedeem_cappedByLiquidity_andRevertPastCap`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L260)
   - [x] negative: paused redeem is rejected at the ERC-4626 max guard — [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`testRevert_Paused_VaultRedeemRejectedAtMaxGuard`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L203)
   - [x] boundaries: deposit then redeem returns no more than deposited — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`testFuzz_roundTrip_depositRedeem_noFreeProfit`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L145)
   - [x] state effects: full redeem drains managed shares to zero, credit stays unclaimed — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol): [`test_withdraw_partialThenFull_tracksManaged`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L369), [`test_decimals_below18_donationImmuneAndTracks`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L473)
   - [x] positive: redeem on behalf spends allowance for exactly the redeemed shares — [`test_redeem_onBehalf_spendsAllowanceForRedeemedShares`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L784)
   - [x] boundaries: after interest the only holder's full redeem leaves managed core shares, here worth one raw unit, in an empty vault — [`test_fullRedeemAfterInterest_leavesManagedCoreSharesInEmptyVault`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L218)
7. [`settle(uint256,address[],address,address)`](src/LidoLendERC4626.sol#L220)
   - [x] positive: cuts each listed borrower to its personal target, collateral to the receiver — [`test_settle_liquidityNeutral`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L518), [`test_settle_receiverNotOwner`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L662); [`test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol): [`test_settle_threeBorrowersStopAtPersonalTargets`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L408)
   - [x] positive: uses only the personal slice, never the larger queue-root quote — [`test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol): [`test_settle_actualRootWithLargerQueueQuote_usesOnlyPersonal`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol#L279); [`test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol): [`test_settle_multipleBorrowersStopAtPersonalTargets`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L365)
   - [x] positive: skips a listed borrower with no slice and settles the next — [`test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol): [`test_settle_nonRootReverts_thenEligibleViaPersonalLtv`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol#L635)
   - [x] negative: reverts `EmptyBorrowers` on an empty list — [`testRevert_settle_emptyBorrowers`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L480)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroShares` on zero shares — [`testRevert_settle_zeroShares`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L486)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroAddress` on a zero receiver — [`testRevert_settle_zeroReceiver`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L493)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ExceededBalance` above the owner's balance — [`testRevert_settle_exceededBalance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L647); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol): [`test_settle_sharesAboveBalance_reverts`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol#L136)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroMorphoSupplyShares` when the redemption budget is zero core shares — [`testRevert_settle_zeroMorphoSupplyShares`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L502)
   - [x] negative: reverts `NothingSettled` when no listed borrower has a positive personal slice — [`test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol): [`testRevert_settle_rootWithoutPersonalLtv_settlesNothing`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol#L327); [`test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol): [`test_settle_dustBelowMinimumPersonalLtvRevertsNothingSettled`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L334)
   - [x] negative: bad-debt root, pause and disabled settlement revert the call — [`test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol): [`testRevert_settle_whenRootIsBadDebt`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol#L227); [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`testRevert_Paused_SettleRejected`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L160); [`test/scenario/SettlementDisabledMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettlementDisabledMarket.t.sol): [`testRevert_VaultSettle_SettlementDisabled`](test/scenario/SettlementDisabledMarket.t.sol#L76)
   - [x] negative: on behalf without allowance reverts `ERC20InsufficientAllowance` — [`testRevert_settle_onBehalfWithoutAllowance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L682)
   - [x] positive: proceeds on a frozen market — [`test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_FrozenMarket_SettleStillProceeds`](test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol#L54)
   - [x] boundaries: a two-asset budget settles nothing, three assets seize one unit — [`test/scenario/SettleSeizeFloor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleSeizeFloor.t.sol): [`testRevert_vaultSettle_boundaryBudgetSettlesNothing`](test/scenario/SettleSeizeFloor.t.sol#L167), [`test_vaultSettle_sameBudgetSettlesAtParityPrice`](test/scenario/SettleSeizeFloor.t.sol#L185)
   - [x] boundaries: budget below the personal quote caps the borrow cut — [`test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol): [`test_settle_budgetBelowPersonalQuoteCapsBorrowCut`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L246); [`test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol): [`test_settle_budgetLimitedPayoutFitsRedeem`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol#L67)
   - [x] boundaries: budget is `previewRedeem` value even when liquidity covers it (6 decimals) — [`test_settle_usesRedemptionBudget_withSixDecimalLoanToken`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L332)
   - [x] boundaries: a short borrower list burns the settled fraction, rounded up — [`test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol): [`test_settle_borrowerLimitedPayoutUsesActualBurn`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol#L76); [`test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_settlementRounding`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L225)
   - [x] state effects: payout never exceeds the burned shares' redemption value — [`test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol): [`testFuzz_settle_payoutFitsRedeem`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol#L119), [`test_settle_multipleBorrowersPayoutFitsRedeem`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol#L84)
   - [x] state effects: budget ignores core credits; self-settling extracts nothing — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol): [`test_settlementBudget_excludesDonation`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L294), [`test_settlementExtraction_personalTarget_cancelsOnlyOwnShareValue`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L349)
   - [x] state effects: managed shares fall by exactly the core supply burn — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol): [`test_settle_borrowAnchored_tracksManaged`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L395)
   - [x] state effects: free market liquidity is unchanged — [`test_settle_liquidityNeutral`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L518); [`test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol): [`test_settle_withPersonalLtv_ignoresFreeLiquidity`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol#L253)
   - [x] state effects: on behalf spends allowance by exactly the burned shares — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol): [`test_settle_onBehalf_spendsExactAllowance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol#L312)
8. [`fallbackExit(uint256,address,address,uint256)`](src/LidoLendERC4626.sol#L284)
   - [x] positive: liquidity covering the request burns exactly `shares`, no settle leg — [`test_fallbackExit_pureRedeem_whenLiquidityCovers`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L433), [`test_fallbackExit_pureRedeem_matchesPreviewRedeem`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L805)
   - [x] positive: pays all free liquidity, then settles the remainder — [`test_fallbackExit_redeemThenSettle_whenLiquidityShort`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L444); [`test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_drainsLiquidityBeforeApplyingRootPolicy`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol#L220)
   - [x] positive: with zero liquidity the settle leg services the whole exit — [`test_fallbackExit_pureSettle_whenNoIdle`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L533)
   - [x] positive: closes the root borrow-anchored; unaffordable full close falls back to partial — [`test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_closesRootBorrowAnchored`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L228), [`test_fallbackExit_unaffordableFullCloseFallsBackToPartial`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L466)
   - [x] positive: liquid request exits under a bad-debt root; settlement-disabled market too — [`test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol): [`test_FallbackExit_BadDebtRoot_LiquidRequestStillExits`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol#L137); [`test/scenario/SettlementDisabledMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettlementDisabledMarket.t.sol): [`test_FallbackExitCoveredByLiquidity_SucceedsWithoutSettleLeg`](test/scenario/SettlementDisabledMarket.t.sol#L105)
   - [x] positive: proceeds on a frozen market — [`test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_FrozenMarket_FallbackExitStillProceeds`](test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol#L68)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroShares` on zero shares — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_zeroShares`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L465)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroAddress` on a zero receiver — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_zeroReceiver`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L470)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ExceededBalance` above the owner's balance — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_exceededBalance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L634)
   - [x] negative: paused positive-value exit reverts `NothingSettled`, burns nothing — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_doesNotNoopPositiveValueShares_whenPaused`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L387)
   - [x] negative: zero settle budget reverts `ZeroMorphoSupplyShares` and rolls back the liquid leg — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_doesNotBurnZeroValueShareDust_whenPaused`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L405); [`test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol): [`testRevert_fallbackExit_zeroBudgetRollsBackLiquidLeg`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol#L190)
   - [x] negative: controller or core settle error reverts the whole call — [`test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol): [`testRevert_FallbackExit_BadDebtRoot_SmallCutHitsBadDebtGate`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol#L161), [`testRevert_FallbackExit_BadDebtRoot_FullCloseExceedsCollateral`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol#L185); [`test/scenario/SettlementDisabledMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettlementDisabledMarket.t.sol): [`testRevert_FallbackExitNeedingSettleLeg_RevertsWholeCall`](test/scenario/SettlementDisabledMarket.t.sol#L127)
   - [x] negative: caller without enough allowance reverts `ERC20InsufficientAllowance` — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_callerWithoutAllowance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L620); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_onBehalf_settleLeg_insufficientAllowance_reverts`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol#L286)
   - [x] boundaries: request on the last liquid plateau burns the exact shares — [`test_fallbackExit_burnsExactShares_onSixDecimalRedeemPlateau`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L286)
   - [x] boundaries: a zero-value dust share burns without touching core — [`test_fallbackExit_burnsZeroValueShareDust_withoutSettlement`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L356)
   - [x] boundaries: `maxIterations` caps the settle leg into a partial exit — [`test_fallbackExit_maxIterations_capsTheSettleLeg`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L556); [`test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_partialCutUsesBorrowShares`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L451)
   - [x] boundaries: `maxIterations = 0` uses the gas-sized default bound — [`test_fallbackExit_pureSettle_whenNoIdle`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L533); [`test/scenario/FallbackExitGasBenchmark.t.sol`](test/scenario/FallbackExitGasBenchmark.t.sol): [`test_gasCapped_partialRekeyBook_defaultBound`](test/scenario/FallbackExitGasBenchmark.t.sol#L109)
   - [x] boundaries: no executable cut partial-fills on the liquid leg — [`test/scenario/SettleSeizeFloor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleSeizeFloor.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_boundaryTail_partialFillsLiquidLeg`](test/scenario/SettleSeizeFloor.t.sol#L202); [`test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_dustRootPartialFillsUntilDustIsRepaid`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L632)
   - [x] boundaries: budget above or below the second full close sets the cut — [`test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_budgetAboveSecondFullCloseUsesBorrowAnchor`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L486), [`test_fallbackExit_budgetBelowSecondFullCloseCapsBorrowCut`](test/scenario/SettleVaultBorrowAnchor.t.sol#L512)
   - [x] boundaries: the last lender exits fully; virtual shares keep backing — [`test/scenario/FallbackExitDustRepro.t.sol`](test/scenario/FallbackExitDustRepro.t.sol): [`test_regression_lastLenderCanExitOnPopulatedMarket`](test/scenario/FallbackExitDustRepro.t.sol#L25); [`test/scenario/SettleWholePoolFullClose.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettleWholePoolFullClose.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_fullPool_preservesVirtualBacking`](test/scenario/SettleWholePoolFullClose.t.sol#L154)
   - [x] state effects: both legs pay the receiver, not the owner — [`test_fallbackExit_receiverNotOwner`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L591)
   - [x] state effects: payout never exceeds the burned shares' redemption value — [`test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_illiquidPayoutFitsRedeem`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol#L95), [`test_fallbackExit_mixedPartialPayoutUsesWholeCallQuote`](test/scenario/VaultSettlementRedemption.t.sol#L103)
   - [x] state effects: managed shares fall by exactly the core position drop — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_borrowAnchored_tracksManaged`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L421), [`test_fallbackExit_bothLegs_tracksManaged`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L443); [`test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_managedSharesDropByBudget_usdc`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol#L99)
   - [x] state effects: on behalf spends allowance by exactly the burned shares — [`test_fallbackExit_callerWithAllowance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L603); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_onBehalf_settleLeg_spendsExactAllowance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol#L154)
9. share ERC-20 (inherited): `transfer`, `transferFrom`, `approve`
   - [x] positive: share transfers still work while the market is paused — [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_Paused_VaultShareTransferStillWorks`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L271)

   - [x] boundaries: `maxRedeem` is zero at zero liquidity, even for zero-value dust — [`test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol): [`test_maxRedeem_zeroLiquidityDustHolder_isZero`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol#L133)
   - [x] positive: `maxDeposit` and `maxMint` are the sentinel when uncapped, also while frozen — [`test_maxDeposit_maxWhenUncapped`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L856), [`test_maxDeposit_unaffectedByFreeze`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L874)
   - [x] positive: `maxDeposit` is the supply-cap headroom — [`test_maxDeposit_capHeadroomIsExactDepositBoundary`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L888); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`test_state_supplyCap_maxDepositReflectsHeadroom`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L408)
   - [x] positive: `maxMint` is capped `maxDeposit` floored into shares — [`test_maxMint_mirrorsCappedMaxDeposit`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L914); [`test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol): [`test_maxMint_underSupplyCap_isTheShareConversion`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol#L207)
   - [x] negative: all four are zero while paused — [`test_maxDeposit_zeroWhenPaused`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L863); [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_Paused_VaultLimitsAreAllZero`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L176)
   - [x] state effects: stay honoured bounds under random action sequences — [`test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_maxAreHonouredBounds`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L161), [`invariant_maxDepositMintReflectState`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L182)
   - [x] boundaries: a one-unit cap headroom reports a deposit that mints no share, and after a near-total write-off a deposit under `maxDeposit` overflows the core's share total — [`testRevert_dustCapHeadroom_depositOfMaxDepositMintsZeroShares`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L994), [`testRevert_afterNearTotalWriteOff_depositUnderMaxDepositOverflowsCoreShares`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L1024)
11. preview and convert views: `previewDeposit`, `previewMint`, `previewWithdraw`, `previewRedeem`, `ERC4626.convertToShares`, `ERC4626.convertToAssets`
   - [x] positive: `convertTo*` equals the matching preview and is caller-invariant — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`testFuzz_convert_equalsPreview_andCallerInvariant`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L129)
   - [x] boundaries: `previewMint` rounds up and `previewRedeem` down, differing by at most one — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`testFuzz_previewRounding_directions`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L185)
   - [x] negative: no view reverts while paused or frozen — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`test_state_paused_disablesEverything`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L380), [`test_state_frozen_lenderFlowsUnaffected`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L362)
12. accounting views: [`totalAssets`](src/LidoLendERC4626.sol#L141), [`managedSupplyShares`](src/LidoLendERC4626.sol#L130)
   - [x] positive: `totalAssets` rises by the deposited assets — [`test_totalAssets_tracksDeposit`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L834)
   - [x] negative: a direct token donation leaves `totalAssets` and price unchanged — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`test_donation_isUncounted_priceUnmoved`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L247); [`test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_donationNeverMovesPrice`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L252)
   - [x] boundaries: zero with no managed shares despite a core credit — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol): [`test_donationBeforeFirstDeposit_doesNotBackShares`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L119)
   - [x] state effects: values pending interest; core fee shares stay unmanaged — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol): [`test_pendingFeeShares_doNotTransferValueToEntrant`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626AuditFixes.t.sol#L193)
   - [x] state effects: never exceeds the accrued market supply — [`test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_noValueCreation`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L128)
13. config views: [`marketParams`](src/LidoLendERC4626.sol#L125), [`marketId`](src/LidoLendERC4626.sol#L63), [`controller`](src/LidoLendERC4626.sol#L66)
   - [x] positive: `marketParams` returns the configured params — [`test_marketParams_returnsConfigured`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L843)
   - [x] positive: `controller` and `marketId` return the bound values — [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol): [`test_deployERC4626Wrapper_acceptsCanonicalControllerForCreatedMarket`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L858)

#### Events
1. [`Settled(address,address,address,uint256,uint256,uint256)`](src/interfaces/ILidoLendERC4626.sol#L102)
   - [x] emitted once with caller, owner, receiver and recomputed amounts — [`test_settle_liquidityNeutral`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L518); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol): [`test_settle_onBehalf_spendsExactAllowance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol#L312)
2. [`FallbackExit(address,address,address,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256,uint256)`](src/interfaces/ILidoLendERC4626.sol#L84)
   - [x] emitted with the liquid and settle legs split — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_bothLegs_eventSplitsTheLegs`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol#L210)
   - [x] emitted with a zero settle leg on a fully liquid exit — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_fullyLiquid_emitsEventWithZeroSettleLeg`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol#L252)
   - [x] emitted per resumed on-behalf call with settle-leg-only amounts — [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_onBehalf_settleLeg_spendsExactAllowance`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Exits.t.sol#L154)
3. `Deposit(address,address,uint256,uint256)`, `Withdraw(address,address,address,uint256,uint256)` (inherited)
   - [x] emitted with the exact assets and shares — [`test_depositWithdraw_roundTrip`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626.t.sol#L195); [`test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol): [`test_withdraw_onBehalf_spendsAllowance_paysReceiver`](test/unit/LidoLendERC4626Conformance.t.sol#L196)

### `ExitBookBase` — [`src/periphery/ExitBookBase.sol`](src/periphery/ExitBookBase.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/ExitBookBase.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookBase.t.sol), [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol), [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol), [`test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol)

1. [`constructor(address,uint256)`](src/periphery/ExitBookBase.sol#L202)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroAddress` on a zero admin — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_constructor_zeroAdmin`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1205)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroMaxLifetime` on a zero lifetime — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_constructor_zeroMaxLifetime`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1200)
   - [x] state effects: `admin` holds `DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE` and `MAX_REQUEST_LIFETIME` is stored — [`test_constructor_storesAdminAndLifetime`](test/unit/ExitBookBase.t.sol#L46)
2. [`setServiceFee(address,uint256)`](src/periphery/ExitBookBase.sol#L225)
   - [x] positive: stores recipient and fee, emits `ServiceFeeSet` — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_setServiceFee_happyPath`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1227)
   - [x] negative: reverts `FeeTooHigh` above `MAX_SERVICE_FEE` — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_setServiceFee_feeTooHigh`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1213)
   - [x] negative: reverts `FeeRecipientRequired` on a nonzero fee with zero recipient — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_setServiceFee_recipientRequired`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1219)
   - [x] boundaries: zero fee with zero recipient clears both — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_setServiceFee_happyPath`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1227)
   - [x] boundaries: exactly `MAX_SERVICE_FEE` is accepted and applied — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_fill_conservation_maxServiceFee_plateauRate`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L383)
   - [x] access control: non-`FEE_MANAGER_ROLE` caller reverts — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_setServiceFee_unauthorized`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1252)
   - [x] positive: still settable while paused — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_pauseAndResumeAreIdempotentAndGatePlacementAndFill`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1292)
3. [`pause()`](src/periphery/ExitBookBase.sol#L241)
   - [x] positive: sets `paused`, emits `Paused`, idempotent — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_pauseAndResumeAreIdempotentAndGatePlacementAndFill`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1292)
   - [x] state effects: blocks placement and fills, rolls back state — [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`test_bookPause_blocksPlacementWithoutCreatingRequest`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L1004), [`test_bookPause_blocksFillAndPreservesState`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L1022)
   - [x] state effects: cancellation still works while paused — [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`test_bookPause_allowsCancellation`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L1054); [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_cancelExitRequest_marksCancelledAndEmits`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1343)
   - [x] access control: non-`PAUSER_ROLE` caller reverts — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_pause_unauthorized`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1272)
4. [`resume()`](src/periphery/ExitBookBase.sol#L247)
   - [x] positive: clears `paused`, emits `Unpaused`, idempotent, fills resume — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_pauseAndResumeAreIdempotentAndGatePlacementAndFill`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1292)
   - [x] access control: non-`RESUMER_ROLE` caller reverts — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_resume_unauthorized`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1280)
5. [`fillExitRequest(uint256,uint256,address)`](src/periphery/ExitBookBase.sol#L258)
   - [x] positive: pays receiver, reward recipient and fee recipient; three legs sum to the fill — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_fill_withYieldRetainsLenderYieldAndRoutesReward`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L331), [`testFuzz_fill_threeWayConservation`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L394); [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`test_fill_pushesPayouts`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L265)
   - [x] positive: zero service fee pays the whole priority fee to the reward recipient — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_fill_conservation_noYield_noFee`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L376)
   - [x] positive: priority rate holds, ramps linearly, then plateaus — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_priorityRamp_holdLinearPlateau`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1029)
   - [x] negative: reverts `EnforcedPause` while paused — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_pauseAndResumeAreIdempotentAndGatePlacementAndFill`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1292)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroAddress` on a zero reward recipient — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_fill_zeroRewardRecipient`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L794)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroAssets` on a zero fill — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_fill_zeroExactAmount`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L786)
   - [x] negative: reverts `RequestClosed` on a cancelled request — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_cancelExitRequest_marksCancelledAndEmits`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1343)
   - [x] negative: closed or expired fills revert and roll back — [`test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol): [`test_seededCriticalPaths_areNonVacuous`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L154)
   - [x] negative: reverts `FillExceedsRemaining` above the remainder — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_fill_frontRunReducedRemainderRejectsWithoutTouchingExecutor`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L768)
   - [x] boundaries: `FillBelowMinimum` below the minimum; the whole final tail may undercut it — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_minFill_usesDirectAssetBoundaryAndAllowsOnlyTheWholeFinalTail`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1086); [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`test_partialFill_borrowerDrainsIdle`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L579)
   - [x] negative: reverts `AssetsOutMismatch` on excess or zero source output — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_fill_excessAssetsOutRollsBackWithdrawalAndDebit`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L921), [`test_fill_noAssetsWithdrawn_reverts`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1324)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ShareBurnExceedsMaximum` above the per-fill ceil limit — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_maxShareBurn_partialBurnAboveCeilLimitRollsBack`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L580), [`testRevert_maxShareBurn_lossRollsBackWithdrawalAndRequest`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L655)
   - [x] boundaries: a burn exactly at the full-fill limit passes — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_maxShareBurn_fullFillAtExactLimitPasses`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L550)
   - [x] boundaries: split fills may exceed `maxShareBurn` by one unit per extra fill — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_maxShareBurn_splitFillsCanExceedNominalBudgetWithinCeilBound`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L464), [`testFuzz_maxShareBurn_splitPartitionReachesCumulativeCeilBound`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L500)
   - [x] boundaries: `maxShareBurn = 0` disables the limit under a source loss — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_fill_conservation_withLoss`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L636)
   - [x] negative: a failed payout transfer rolls back the whole fill — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_fill_serviceCutTransferFailureRollsBackEntireFill`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L935)
   - [x] state effects: debits exactly the fill and marks `Filled` at zero remainder — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_fill_partialSlicesWithdrawExactAssetsAndRetainYield`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L616), [`test_fill_afterYieldBurnsLiveSharesAndStillPaysExactAssets`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L688)
   - [x] state effects: never uses executor assets; pre-existing book balance is ignored — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_fill_doesNotUseExecutorAssetsWhenSourceIsLiquid`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L814), [`test_fill_ignoresPreExistingBookDonationWhenMeasuringExactOutput`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L908)
   - [x] state effects: the book holds no assets at rest — [`test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_bookHoldsNoAssets`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L119), [`invariant_payoutConservation`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L129)
6. [`cancelExitRequest(uint256)`](src/periphery/ExitBookBase.sol#L316)
   - [x] positive: marks `Cancelled`, zeroes the remainder, emits `ExitCancelled` — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_cancelExitRequest_marksCancelledAndEmits`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1343)
   - [x] negative: reverts `NotOwner` for a non-owner — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_cancelExitRequest_notOwner`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1363)
   - [x] boundaries: an expired open request can still be cancelled — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_cancelAfterExpiryClosesTheRequest`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1371)
   - [x] negative: cancelling an already-closed request through the book reverts `RequestClosed` — [`testRevert_cancelExitRequest_alreadyClosed`](test/unit/ExitBookBase.t.sol#L68)

   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroAssets` on zero assets — [`testRevert_createRequest_zeroAssets`](test/unit/ExitBookBase.t.sol#L39)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroAddress` on a zero receiver — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_place_zeroReceiver`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1152)

   - [x] negative: reverts `PriorityFeeTooHigh` above `MAX_PRIORITY_FEE` — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_place_endFeeTooHigh`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1124)
   - [x] negative: reverts `StartFeeAboveEnd` when the start rate exceeds the end — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_place_startFeeAboveEnd`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1113)
   - [x] negative: reverts `RampStartAfterEnd` when the ramp starts after it ends — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_place_rampStartAfterEnd`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1384)
   - [x] negative: reverts `DeadlineInPast` at the current timestamp — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_place_deadlineInPast`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1395)
   - [x] negative: reverts `DeadlineTooFar` one second past the lifetime — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_place_deadlineTooFar`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1402)
   - [x] negative: reverts `RampPastDeadline` when the ramp ends after the deadline — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`testRevert_place_rampPastDeadline`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1135)
   - [x] boundaries: a deadline exactly `MAX_REQUEST_LIFETIME` away is accepted — [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`testFuzz_place_storesAssetRequestAndEmits`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L347)
   - [x] boundaries: `maxShareBurn` equal to the placement quote is accepted — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_maxShareBurn_fullFillAtExactLimitPasses`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L550)
   - [x] boundaries: `minFillAssets` is capped at the request total — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_place_capsMinimumAtAssetTarget`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1107); [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`testFuzz_place_storesAssetRequestAndEmits`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L347)
   - [x] boundaries: `endRate == MAX_PRIORITY_FEE` is accepted — [`test_createRequest_endRateAtMaxPriorityFee_accepted`](test/unit/ExitBookBase.t.sol#L54)
   - [x] boundaries: `block.timestamp + rampEnd == deadline` is accepted — [`test_createRequest_rampEndAtDeadline_accepted`](test/unit/ExitBookBase.t.sol#L62)

   - [x] negative: reverts `NotAuthorizedOnMorpho` without core authorization — [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`testRevert_place_notAuthorized`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L318)
   - [x] negative: reverts `InsufficientBacking` one asset above the position — [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`testRevert_place_insufficientBacking`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L338)
   - [x] boundaries: backing uses expected balances with pending interest, without accruing — [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`test_place_pendingInterestWithDifferentFeeRecipient_usesExpectedBacking`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L722)

   - [x] boundaries: underbacked request needs a smaller explicit fill — [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`test_badDebtUnderbacking_requiresFillerToChooseSmallerExactFill`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L903), [`test_badDebtLoss_stillFillsExactAssetsWhenBackingRemains`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L781)
5. views: [`marketParamsOf`](src/periphery/MorphoExitBook.sol#L109), [`isMarketAllowed`](src/interfaces/IMorphoExitBook.sol#L17)

   - [x] positive: `isMarketAllowed` reads false after disallow — [`test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol): [`test_disallowMarket_blocksPlaceNotFill`](test/scenario/MorphoExitBook.t.sol#L503)

### `ExitBookLib` — [`src/libs/ExitBookLib.sol`](src/libs/ExitBookLib.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol), [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol), [`test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol)

1. [`add(State,address,address,IERC20,uint256,uint256,uint256,PriorityRamp,uint256)`](src/libs/ExitBookLib.sol#L45)
   - [x] positive: creates an `Open` request with id 1 and the given fields — [`test_add_createsOpenRequest`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L108)
   - [x] state effects: ids increment and are never reused after close — [`test_add_idsMonotonic_neverReused`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L123); [`test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_monotonicIds`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L138)
   - [x] state effects: appends the id to the owner index — [`test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol): [`test_viewsReturnStoredRequestsAndOwnerIndexesWithoutSourcePreviews`](test/unit/ExitBookFeeSplit.t.sol#L1409)
   - [x] boundaries: every packed field accepts its exact maximum — [`test_add_packedFieldsAcceptExactMaxima`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L212)
   - [x] negative: `assets`, `minFillAssets`, `maxShareBurn` above uint128 revert on SafeCast — [`testRevert_add_assetsOverflowUint128`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L230), [`testRevert_add_minFillAssetsOverflowsUint128`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L239), [`testRevert_add_maxShareBurnOverflowsUint128`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L245)
   - [x] negative: `deadline` and `createdAt` above uint40 revert on SafeCast — [`testRevert_add_deadlineOverflowsUint40`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L252), [`testRevert_add_createdAtOverflowsUint40`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L260)
   - [x] negative: ramp rates above uint96 revert on SafeCast — [`testRevert_add_rampRateOverflowsUint96`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L270), [`testRevert_add_endRateOverflowsUint96`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L280)
   - [x] negative: ramp offsets above uint32 revert on SafeCast — [`testRevert_add_rampStartOverflowsUint32`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L288), [`testRevert_add_rampEndOverflowsUint32`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L296)
   - [x] state effects: stored `createdAt`, `deadline` and ramp fields read back unchanged — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_shortRun_repaymentUnwind`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L85)
2. [`recordFill(State,uint256,uint256)`](src/libs/ExitBookLib.sol#L77)
   - [x] positive: a partial fill debits the remainder and stays `Open` — [`test_recordFill_partial_staysOpen`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L136)
   - [x] boundaries: a fill draining the remainder marks `Filled` — [`test_recordFill_full_marksFilled`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L144)
   - [x] negative: an overfill reverts on underflow — [`testRevert_recordFill_overfill`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L152)
   - [x] negative: reverts `RequestClosed` on a cancelled or filled request — [`testRevert_recordFill_onCancelled_revertsClosed`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L194), [`testRevert_recordFill_onFilled_revertsClosed`](test/unit/ExitBookLib.t.sol#L202)
