### `LidoLendVaultAdapter` — [`src/adapters/LidoLendVaultAdapter.sol`](src/adapters/LidoLendVaultAdapter.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol), [`test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol`](test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol), [`test/scenario/BundledFlowsDecimals.t.sol`](test/scenario/BundledFlowsDecimals.t.sol)

1. [`constructor(address)`](src/adapters/LidoLendVaultAdapter.sol#L27)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroAddress` on a zero Bundler3 — [`testRevert_constructor_zeroBundler3`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L84)
2. [`fallbackExit(address,uint256,uint256,address,uint256,uint256)`](src/adapters/LidoLendVaultAdapter.sol#L44)
   - [x] positive: the owner is always the initiator, whoever calls — [`test_fallbackExit_ownerAlwaysInitiator`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L96)
   - [x] positive: a receiver other than the initiator is forwarded to the vault — [`test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol`](test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol): [`test_bundle_fallbackExit_paysWethAndUnwrapsCollateralToStEth`](test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol#L217)
   - [x] positive: pays loan and unwraps seized collateral in one bundle — [`test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol`](test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol): [`test_bundle_fallbackExit_paysWethAndUnwrapsCollateralToStEth`](test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol#L217); [`test/scenario/BundledFlowsDecimals.t.sol`](test/scenario/BundledFlowsDecimals.t.sol): [`test_bundle_fallbackExit_sixDecimalLoanToken`](test/scenario/BundledFlowsDecimals.t.sol#L144)
   - [x] access control: a call outside a bundle reverts `UnauthorizedSender` — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_unauthorizedSenderOutsideBundle`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L91)
   - [x] access control: a foreign initiator cannot spend another user's approval — [`test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol`](test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol): [`testRevert_bundle_fallbackExit_foreignInitiatorCannotSpendApproval`](test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol#L278)
   - [x] boundaries: `type(uint256).max` resolves to the initiator's balance — [`test_fallbackExit_maxSentinelUsesInitiatorBalance`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L107)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroShares` on zero shares or an empty sentinel — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_zeroShares`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L116)
   - [x] negative: reverts `ZeroAddress` on a zero receiver — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_zeroReceiver`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L136)
   - [x] boundaries: burn exactly at `minSharesBurned` passes, one below reverts `BurnBelowMinimum` — [`test_fallbackExit_burnFloorBoundary`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L152), [`testRevert_fallbackExit_burnBelowMinimum`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L160)
   - [x] boundaries: loan plus debt exactly at `minExitAssets` passes, below reverts `ExitBelowMinimum` — [`test_fallbackExit_valueFloorBoundaryWithBothLegs`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L179), [`testRevert_fallbackExit_valueBelowFloor`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L187), [`testFuzz_fallbackExit_floorMatchesReturnedAccounting`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L228)
   - [x] negative: the value floor ignores seized collateral quantity — [`testRevert_fallbackExit_floorIgnoresCollateralQuantity`](test/unit/LidoLendVaultAdapter.t.sol#L205)
   - [x] state effects: a floor miss reverts the whole bundle, shares kept — [`test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol`](test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol): [`testRevert_bundle_fallbackExit_belowFloorAtomic`](test/scenario/BundledFlows.t.sol#L253)

### `GuardianSignals` — [`src/GuardianSignals.sol`](src/GuardianSignals.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol), [`test/unit/GuardianSignalsAuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsAuditFixes.t.sol), [`test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol), [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol), [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol), [`test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol)

1. [`constructor()`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L88)
   - [x] negative: `initialize` on the bare implementation reverts (`InvalidInitialization`) — [`testRevert_initialize_bareImplementation`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1479)
   - [x] boundaries: frontier sync on the uninitialized implementation is a no-op — [`test_frontier_uninitializedRegistry_syncIsNoOp`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L701)

2. [`initialize(address,address[],uint256)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L97)
   - [x] positive: seeds the guardian set in order — [`test_getGuardians_returnsFullSet`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1016), [`test_isGuardian_reflectsMembership`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1011)
   - [x] positive: emits `QuorumSet` with the initial quorum — [`test_initialize_emitsQuorumSet`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1447)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero admin (`ZeroAddress`) — [`testRevert_initialize_zeroAdmin`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1460)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero guardian in the set (`ZeroAddress`) — [`testRevert_initialize_zeroGuardianInSet`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1469)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a duplicate guardian in the set (`AlreadyGuardian`) — [`testRevert_initialize_duplicateGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1492)
   - [x] negative: reverts on quorum 0 or above the set size (`BadQuorum`) — [`testRevert_initialize_badQuorum`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1498)
   - [x] negative: reverts on more than 32 guardians (`MaxGuardiansReached`) — [`testRevert_initialize_moreThan32Guardians`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1503)
   - [x] negative: a second `initialize` reverts (`InvalidInitialization`) — [`testRevert_initialize_twice`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1486)
   - [x] state effects: only `admin` holds `DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE`; suspect window starts at 0 — [`test_initialize_onlyAdminHoldsAdminRole_suspectWindowZero`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1511)

3. [`addGuardian(address)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L115)
   - [x] positive: the new guardian gains signal rights — [`test_addGuardian_grantsSignalRights`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L961)
   - [x] negative: reverts on an existing guardian (`AlreadyGuardian`) — [`testRevert_addGuardian_existing`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L942)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero address (`ZeroAddress`) — [`testRevert_addGuardian_zeroAddress`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L947)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a current delegate (`AccountIsDelegate`) — [`testRevert_addGuardian_isDelegate`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L445)
   - [x] boundaries: the 33rd guardian reverts (`MaxGuardiansReached`) — [`testRevert_addGuardian_max32`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L952)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MANAGER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_addGuardian_wrongRole`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L921)

4. [`replaceGuardian(address,address)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L120)
   - [x] positive: swaps a seat at cap and full quorum, count and quorum kept — [`test_replaceGuardian_atCapAndFullQuorum_preservesBoth`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1555)
   - [x] positive: the outgoing guardian's own delegate can take the seat — [`test_replaceGuardian_withOwnDelegate_succeeds`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L464)
   - [x] negative: reverts when the old address is not a guardian (`NotGuardian`) — [`testRevert_replaceGuardian_oldNotGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1587)
   - [x] negative: reverts when the new address is a guardian (`AlreadyGuardian`) — [`testRevert_replaceGuardian_newAlreadyGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1592)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero new guardian (`ZeroAddress`) — [`testRevert_replaceGuardian_zeroNewGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1598)
   - [x] negative: reverts on another guardian's delegate (`AccountIsDelegate`) — [`testRevert_replaceGuardian_newIsDelegate`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L454)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MANAGER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_replaceGuardian_wrongRole`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1603)
   - [x] state effects: clears the old guardian's delegate and heartbeat — [`test_replaceGuardian_clearsOldDelegateAndHeartbeat`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1574)

5. [`removeGuardian(address)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L131)
   - [x] positive: drops the guardian; the ex-guardian can no longer signal — [`test_removeGuardian_succeeds`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L980)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a non-guardian (`NotGuardian`) — [`testRevert_removeGuardian_notGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L997)
   - [x] boundaries: removal to exactly quorum passes; below it reverts (`WouldBreakQuorum`) — [`test_removeGuardian_succeeds`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L980), [`testRevert_removeGuardian_wouldBreakQuorum`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1003)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MANAGER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_removeGuardian_wrongRole`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L928)
   - [x] state effects: clears the delegate and frees it for reuse — [`test_removeGuardian_clearsDelegate`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L503), [`test_setDelegate_freedDelegateIsReusable`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L420)
   - [x] state effects: the removed guardian's flags keep counting until `prune` — [`test_prune_removesRemovedGuardianFlag`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1034)
   - [x] state effects: resets the heartbeat, so a re-added guardian starts at zero — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_frontierMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L124)

6. [`setDelegate(address)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L137)
   - [x] positive: the delegate acts for its guardian; signals are keyed by the guardian — [`test_delegate_actsOnBehalfOfGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L313), [`test_delegate_canUnflagAndSuspect`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L326)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a non-guardian caller (`Unauthorized`) — [`testRevert_setDelegate_nonGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L481)
   - [x] negative: reverts on any guardian address, own included (`DelegateIsGuardian`) — [`testRevert_setDelegate_delegateIsGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L379)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a delegate already taken, own included (`AccountIsDelegate`) — [`testRevert_setDelegate_takenByAnotherGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L399), [`testRevert_setDelegate_reSetOwnDelegate`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L409)
   - [x] boundaries: clearing to zero is not blocked by delegateless guardians — [`test_delegate_clear_notBlockedByOtherDelegatelessGuardians`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L370)
   - [x] state effects: re-pointing or clearing revokes the old delegate — [`test_delegate_reappointRevokesOld`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L342), [`test_delegate_clear`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L356)
   - [x] state effects: a delegate freed by clear, re-point or removal is reusable — [`test_setDelegate_freedDelegateIsReusable`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L420)

7. [`setQuorum(uint256)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L147)
   - [x] positive: quorum equal to the guardian count applies to flags — [`test_setQuorum_equalsCount`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L904)
   - [x] negative: reverts on zero (`BadQuorum`) — [`testRevert_setQuorum_zero`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L894)
   - [x] negative: reverts above the guardian count (`BadQuorum`) — [`testRevert_setQuorum_aboveGuardianCount`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L899)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MANAGER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_setQuorum_wrongRole`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L914)
   - [x] state effects: lowering bans existing strikes; raising lifts a never-latched ban — [`test_derivedBan_tracksQuorumBothWays`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L299); [`test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol): [`test_M1_loweringQuorumBansExistingStrikes`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol#L49), [`test_M1_derivedBanTracksQuorumBothWays`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol#L75)
   - [x] state effects: a latched ban survives a quorum raise — [`test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol): [`test_M1_raisingQuorumLiftsDerivedBan`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol#L63)
   - [x] state effects: a quorum raise never lowers the stored frontier — [`test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol): [`test_SyncFrontier_NeverMovesBackwards`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol#L174)

8. [`setSuspectWindow(uint256)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L153)
   - [x] positive: accepts `MAX_SUSPECT_WINDOW`, emits `SuspectWindowSet`, reads back — [`test_setSuspectWindow_atMaxOk`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1438)
   - [x] negative: reverts above `MAX_SUSPECT_WINDOW` (`SuspectWindowTooHigh`) — [`testRevert_setSuspectWindow_aboveMax`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1432)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MANAGER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_setSuspectWindow_wrongRole`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L935)
   - [x] state effects: widening revives stored lapsed reports without recounting — [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol): [`test_widenedWindow_revivesStoredLapsedReports_delaysTheLot`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol#L328)
   - [x] state effects: widening after cleanup revives nothing — [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol): [`test_unsuspectPrunesExpiredReports_widenedWindowDoesNotReviveCount`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol#L343)

9. [`heartbeat(address,BlockInfoWithHash,bytes)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L198)
   - [x] positive: records the timestamp and emits the payload verbatim — [`test_heartbeat_emitsDataVerbatim`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L840), [`test_heartbeat_emptyData_accepted`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L850), [`testFuzz_heartbeat_dataIsPassThrough`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L860)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a caller neither guardian nor delegate (`Unauthorized`) — [`testRevert_heartbeat_unauthorizedCaller`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L548)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a future block number (`BlockInTheFuture`) — [`testRevert_heartbeat_blockInTheFuture`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L794)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a future timestamp (`TimestampInTheFuture`) — [`testRevert_heartbeat_timestampInTheFuture`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L802)
   - [x] negative: reverts below the previous timestamp (`HeartbeatTimestampRegressed`) — [`testRevert_heartbeat_regressesBelowPrevious`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L810)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a wrong hash, whatever the payload (`InvalidBlockHash`) — [`testRevert_heartbeat_invalidBlockHash`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L819), [`testRevert_heartbeat_dataDoesNotBypassValidation`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L872)
   - [x] negative: rejects a zero hash for the current or an out-of-window block — [`test/unit/GuardianSignalsAuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsAuditFixes.t.sol): [`test_W1_heartbeatRejectsZeroHashCurrentBlock`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsAuditFixes.t.sol#L43), [`test_W1_heartbeatRejectsZeroHashStaleBlock`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsAuditFixes.t.sol#L52)
   - [x] boundaries: timestamps equal to the previous one or to now pass — [`test_heartbeat_equalTimestamps_accepted`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L828); [`test/unit/GuardianSignalsAuditFixes.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsAuditFixes.t.sol): [`test_W1_heartbeatAcceptsCanonicalBlock`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsAuditFixes.t.sol#L63)
   - [x] state effects: the quorum-th heartbeat advances the frontier in the same call — [`test_frontier_heartbeatAdvancesEagerly`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L644)
   - [x] state effects: a heartbeat already above the frontier does not raise it — [`test_frontier_heartbeatAboveFrontier_noAdvance`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L685)
   - [x] state effects: delegate heartbeats are stored under the guardian (fuzzed) — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_frontierMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L124)

10. [`syncQuorumFrontier()`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L226)
    - [x] positive: sets the frontier to the quorum-th largest heartbeat — [`test_frontier_atQuorum_advancesToQuorumthLargest`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L708), [`testFuzz_frontier_isQuorumthLargest`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1635), [`test_syncQuorumFrontier_everyCommitteeSizeAndQuorum_isQuorumthLargest`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L736)
    - [x] access control: callable by anyone — [`test_syncQuorumFrontier_isPermissionless`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L727)
    - [x] boundaries: fewer than quorum heartbeats keep the frontier at zero — [`test_frontier_belowQuorumHeartbeats_noAdvance`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L693)
    - [x] boundaries: quorum 0 on an uninitialized registry is a no-op — [`test_frontier_uninitializedRegistry_syncIsNoOp`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L701)
    - [x] state effects: the frontier never moves back, even after a quorum raise — [`test_frontier_monotonic_doesNotRegress`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L717); [`test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol): [`test_SyncFrontier_NeverMovesBackwards`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol#L174)
    - [x] state effects: the stored frontier matches the spec model across random actions — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_frontierMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L124)

11. [`flag(Id,address,address[])`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L254)
    - [x] positive: a sub-quorum flag is a strike; the quorum-th flag latches the ban — [`test_flag_belowQuorum_isNormalWithStrikes`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L124), [`test_flag_atQuorum_latchesBanned`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L131)
    - [x] negative: reverts for a stranger, a non-guardian or another guardian's `onBehalf` — [`testRevert_flag_unauthorizedCaller`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L515), [`testRevert_flag_onBehalfOfNonGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L522), [`testRevert_flag_onBehalfOfAnotherGuardian`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L529)
    - [x] negative: a re-pointed or cleared delegate reverts (`Unauthorized`) — [`test_delegate_reappointRevokesOld`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L342), [`test_delegate_clear`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L356)
    - [x] negative: a zero account reverts the whole batch (`ZeroAddress`) — [`testRevert_flag_zeroAccountInBatch`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L604)
    - [x] boundaries: a repeat flag by the same guardian adds no strike — [`test_flag_sameGuardianTwice_isIdempotent`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L148)
    - [x] boundaries: a repeat flag emits no second `Flagged` — [`test_flag_sameGuardianTwice_emitsNoSecondFlagged`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L154)
    - [x] state effects: a batch gives each account its own strike — [`test_flag_batch_multipleAccounts`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L593)
    - [x] state effects: does not record a heartbeat — [`test_flag_doesNotHeartbeat`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L622)
    - [x] state effects: ban equals latch or strikes ≥ quorum across random sequences — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_banEqualsLatchOrQuorum`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L98)

12. [`unflag(Id,address,address[])`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L262)
    - [x] positive: drops the strike; a latched ban stays — [`test_latch_unflagBelowQuorum_staysBanned`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L139)
    - [x] positive: a delegate retracts its guardian's flag — [`test_delegate_canUnflagAndSuspect`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L326)
    - [x] negative: reverts for an unauthorized caller (`Unauthorized`) — [`testRevert_unflag_unauthorizedCaller`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L536)
    - [x] boundaries: retracting a flag never cast is a no-op — [`test_unflag_nonFlagger_isNoop`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L167)
    - [x] state effects: latches a quorum-derived ban before dropping the count — [`test_quorumLowering_unflagLatchesDerivedBan`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L254)
    - [x] state effects: once banned, any flag/unflag sequence stays banned — [`testFuzz_latchHoldsAcrossSequence`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1613)

13. [`suspect(Id,address,address[])`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L270)
    - [x] positive: records the report and refreshes the count and expiry snapshot — [`test_suspect_snapshotsCountAndExpiry`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1158)
    - [x] positive: a delegate's report counts for its guardian — [`test_delegate_canUnflagAndSuspect`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L326)
    - [x] negative: reverts on a zero account (`ZeroAddress`) — [`testRevert_suspect_zeroAccount`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L615)
    - [x] negative: a stale guardian or former delegate reverts (`Unauthorized`, fuzzed) — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_registryMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L111)
    - [x] boundaries: a report counts on the last second of its window — [`test_suspect_reportOnItsLastSecond_countsAndIsNotPruned`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1365)
    - [x] boundaries: a lapsed report is pruned and uncounted on the next report — [`test_suspect_expiredReport_prunedAndUncounted`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1378)
    - [x] state effects: never bans — [`test_suspect_neverBans`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1171)
    - [x] state effects: does not record a heartbeat — [`test_suspect_doesNotHeartbeat`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L628)
    - [x] state effects: a refreshed report prunes lapsed ones and recounts — [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol): [`test_staleCount_healedByAFreshReport_shortensTheLot`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol#L228)

14. [`unsuspect(Id,address,address[])`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L278)
    - [x] positive: retraction re-snapshots the count — [`test_unsuspect_reSnapshotsCount`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1180)
    - [x] negative: reverts for an unauthorized caller (`Unauthorized`) — [`testRevert_unsuspect_unauthorizedCaller`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L542)
    - [x] state effects: the other live report and its expiry remain — [`test_unsuspectFirst_bothReportsLive_leavesSecondReport`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1213), [`test_unsuspectSecond_bothReportsLive_leavesFirstReport`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1238)
    - [x] state effects: prunes stale reports, then rebuilds or clears the snapshot — [`test_unsuspectFirst_firstReportStale_rebuildsSnapshotFromSecondReport`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1269), [`test_unsuspectSecond_firstReportStale_clearsSnapshot`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1295), [`test_unsuspectSecond_bothReportsStale_clearsSnapshot`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1333)
    - [x] state effects: does not extend the snapshot expiry — [`test_unsuspect_doesNotExtendSnapshotExpiry`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1195)
    - [x] boundaries: a caller with no report still prunes lapsed reports — [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol): [`test_unsuspectPrunesExpiredReports_widenedWindowDoesNotReviveCount`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol#L343)

15. [`prune(Id,address)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L288)
    - [x] positive: anyone removes a departed guardian's flag — [`test_prune_removesRemovedGuardianFlag`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1034)
    - [x] positive: removes a departed guardian's suspect report and re-snapshots — [`test_prune_removesRemovedGuardianSuspect`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1395)
    - [x] boundaries: an account with no signals is a no-op — [`test_prune_noSignals_isNoop`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1149)
    - [x] state effects: keeps current guardians' flags — [`test_prune_keepsCurrentGuardianFlags`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1140)
    - [x] state effects: never lifts a latched ban — [`test_prune_doesNotLiftBan`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1129)
    - [x] state effects: a derived ban lapses below quorum and latches at quorum — [`test_prune_derivedBan_lapsesWhenBelowQuorum`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1054), [`test_prune_derivedBan_latchesWhenStillQuorum`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1114)
    - [x] state effects: does not extend the snapshot expiry — [`test_prune_doesNotExtendSnapshotExpiry`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1413)
    - [x] state effects: the rebuild also drops a live guardian's lapsed report from the count — [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol): [`test_staleCount_healedByPruningADepartedGuardian_shortensTheLot`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol#L255)
    - [x] state effects: shrinks a flag set grown past the cap to committee size — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`test_prune_restoresTheBoundAfterRotationExceedsTheCap`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L194)

16. [`ban(Id,address)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L323)
    - [x] positive: latches with zero flags and leaves flags untouched — [`test_forcedBan_latchesRegardlessOfFlags`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L190)
    - [x] negative: reverts on a zero account (`ZeroAddress`) — [`testRevert_ban_zeroAccount`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L244)
    - [x] access control: reverts without `ACCESS_LIMITER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_ban_wrongRole`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L236)
    - [x] state effects: a forced ban survives flag and quorum changes (fuzzed) — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_banEqualsLatchOrQuorum`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L98)

17. [`unban(Id,address)`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L331)
    - [x] positive: lifts the ban and clears flags; a fresh quorum re-bans — [`test_ban_unban_clearsFlagSet`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L176)
    - [x] positive: leaves no residual derived ban — [`test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol): [`test_M1_unbanClearsDerivedBan`](test/unit/GuardianSignalsQuorumBan.t.sol#L89)
    - [x] positive: lifts a forced `ban` that has no flags — [`test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol): [`test_governanceFacade_dispatchesGuardianActionsAcrossRealMarkets`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol#L132)
    - [x] access control: reverts without `ACCESS_RESTORER_ROLE` (fork only) — [`test/fork/GuardianSignalsFlow.t.sol`](test/fork/GuardianSignalsFlow.t.sol): [`testRevert_unban_byAgentDirectly`](test/fork/GuardianSignalsFlow.t.sol#L83)
    - [x] state effects: suspect reports survive the unban (fuzzed model) — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_registryMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L111)
    - [x] state effects: the borrower's lot gets a finite unlock time again — [`test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol): [`test_UnbanRestoresFiniteUnlock_ThenLotMatures`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol#L203)

18. views: [`getQuorum`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L160), [`getSuspectWindow`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L165), [`isGuardian`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L170), [`getDelegate`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L175), [`getGuardianCount`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L180), [`getGuardians`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L185), [`getHeartbeat`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L231), [`getHeartbeats`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L236), [`getQuorumFrontier`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L245), [`getOpinion`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L343), [`isBanned`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L350), [`getFlaggedBy`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L360), [`getSuspectedBy`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L365)
    - [x] positive: `getGuardians`/`getDelegate` pair the set with delegates by index — [`test_getGuardians_pairsSetWithDelegates`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L488)
    - [x] positive: `getHeartbeats` pairs the set with timestamps — [`test_getHeartbeats_pairsSetWithTimestamps`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L881)
    - [x] positive: `getSuspectedBy` pairs reporters with timestamps — [`test_getSuspectedBy_pairsSetWithTimestamps`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1353)
    - [x] positive: `getFlaggedBy` and `isGuardian` reflect stored state — [`test_getFlaggedBy_returnsFlaggerSet`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1024), [`test_isGuardian_reflectsMembership`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1011)
    - [x] state effects: `isBanned` and `getOpinion.banned` equal latch or strikes ≥ quorum — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_banEqualsLatchOrQuorum`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L98)
    - [x] state effects: `getOpinion` matches the model; expiry uses the current window — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_registryMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L111)
    - [x] boundaries: reads never rebuild, so a lapsed report under a live one counts — [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol): [`test_staleCount_lapsedReportUnderALiveOne_delaysTheLot`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol#L198)

6. [`Heartbeat(address,uint256,uint256,bytes)`](src/interfaces/IGuardianSignals.sol#L71)

7. [`QuorumFrontierAdvanced(address,uint256)`](src/interfaces/IGuardianSignals.sol#L76)

9. [`Unflagged(Id,address,address,address,uint256)`](src/interfaces/IGuardianSignals.sol#L118)

10. [`FlagPruned(Id,address,address,uint256)`](src/interfaces/IGuardianSignals.sol#L127)

### `LidoLendMarketFactory` — [`src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol), [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol), [`test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol`](test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol)

1. [`constructor(Implementations,Dependencies,address)`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L327)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero admin (`AdminZeroAddress`) — [`testRevert_constructor_rejectsZeroAdmin`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L221); [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`testRevert_constructor_zeroAdmin`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L401)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero circuit breaker or reseal manager — [`testRevert_constructor_rejectsZeroCircuitBreaker`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L230), [`testRevert_constructor_rejectsZeroResealManager`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L238)
   - [x] negative: reverts when both emergency recipients coincide (`DuplicateEmergencyRoleRecipient`) — [`testRevert_constructor_rejectsDuplicateEmergencyRoleRecipient`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L247)
   - [x] negative: reverts when a controller or wrapper impl uses another core (`LidoLendMismatch`) — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`testRevert_constructor_controllerImplementationUsesAnotherCore`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L342), [`testRevert_constructor_erc4626WrapperImplementationUsesAnotherCore`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L358)
   - [x] negative: reverts when facade, dashboard or IRM router uses another core — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`testRevert_constructor_lidoLendOwnerUsesAnotherCore`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L386), [`testRevert_constructor_riskStewardDashboardUsesAnotherCore`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L374), [`testRevert_constructor_routerUsesAnotherCore`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L309)
   - [x] boundaries: a codeless circuit breaker is accepted and stored — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`test_constructor_doesNotValidateCircuitBreakerCode`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L406)

2. [`deployMarketComponents(MarketSpec,ERC4626WrapperSpec)`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L365)
   - [x] positive: deploys an inert, fully role-wired controller and wrapper — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`test_deployMarketComponents_producesInertWiredComponents`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L99)
   - [x] positive: a second market reuses the implementations behind new proxies — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`test_deployMarketComponents_secondMarketReusesImplementations`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L232)
   - [x] negative: reverts on lltv equal to WAD (`InvalidLltv`) — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`testRevert_deployMarketComponents_invalidLltv`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L300)
   - [x] negative: reverts when the market already exists (`MarketAlreadyExists`) — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`testRevert_deployMarketComponents_marketAlreadyExists`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L264)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a codeless oracle or guardian registry (`NotContract`) — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`testRevert_deployMarketComponents_nonContractOracle`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L279), [`testRevert_deployMarketComponents_invalidGuardianProxy`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L291)
   - [x] negative: an identical redeploy by the same caller collides — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`testRevert_deployMarketComponents_rejectsAnIdenticalRedeploy`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L221)
   - [x] boundaries: lltv of WAD − 1 is accepted — [`test_deployMarketComponents_lltvJustBelowWad_accepted`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L909)
   - [x] state effects: addresses are caller-scoped; a front-runner cannot take them — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`test_deployMarketComponents_addressesSurviveAnotherCallerDeployingFirst`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L200)
   - [x] state effects: still works after core ownership leaves the facade — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`test_deployMarketComponents_afterCoreOwnershipMoves_succeeds`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L412)

3. [`deployMarketController(MarketSpec)`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L381)
   - [x] positive: wires both pausers and the reseal-manager resumer — [`test_deployMarketController_assignsResealManagerEmergencyRoles`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L265)
   - [x] positive: the deployed controller passes `validateMarketController` — [`test_validateMarketController_acceptsExactRoleSets`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L283)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a codeless loan or collateral token (`NotContract`) — [`testRevert_deployMarketController_rejectsCodelessLoanToken`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L257), [`testRevert_deployMarketController_rejectsCodelessCollateralToken`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L275)
   - [x] events: a standalone call emits `MarketControllerDeployed` only — [`test_deployMarketController_standalone_emitsOnlyControllerDeployed`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L926)

4. [`deployERC4626Wrapper(address,MarketParams,ERC4626WrapperSpec)`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L392)
   - [x] positive: a second wrapper over a live canonical controller works at once — [`test_deployERC4626Wrapper_acceptsCanonicalControllerForCreatedMarket`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L858); [`test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol`](test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol): [`test_deployERC4626Wrapper_worksImmediatelyOnActivatedMarket`](test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol#L67)
   - [x] positive: the second wrapper shares the market supply cap — [`test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol`](test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol): [`test_deployERC4626Wrapper_sharesSupplyCapWithOriginalVault`](test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol#L88)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a controller bound to another core (`LidoLendMismatch`) — [`testRevert_deployERC4626Wrapper_rejectsControllerBoundToAnotherCore`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L832)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a non-canonical controller of a created market — [`testRevert_deployERC4626Wrapper_rejectsNonCanonicalControllerForCreatedMarket`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L844)
   - [x] negative: reverts on another market's params (`MarketIdMismatch`) — [`test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol`](test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol): [`testRevert_deployERC4626Wrapper_marketParamsFromAnotherMarket`](test/scenario/DeployVault.t.sol#L120)
   - [x] negative: an identical wrapper (same metadata, same caller) collides — [`testRevert_deployERC4626Wrapper_identicalWrapperCollides`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L948)

5. [`deployGuardianSignals(GuardianSpec,bytes32)`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L408)
   - [x] positive: deploys a configured registry with the exact role wiring — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`test_deployGuardianSignals_producesWiredRegistry`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L476)
   - [x] positive: identical specs under different salts get distinct, predicted addresses — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`test_deployGuardianSignals_identicalSpecsSeparatedBySalt`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L428)
   - [x] negative: a reused salt for the same spec and caller collides — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`testRevert_deployGuardianSignals_rejectsAReusedSalt`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L445)
   - [x] negative: an invalid spec (duplicate guardian, bad quorum, oversized window) reverts — [`testRevert_deployGuardianSignals_invalidSpec`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L976)
   - [x] state effects: the same salt from another caller lands elsewhere — [`test_deployGuardianSignals_sameSaltOtherCaller_landsElsewhere`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L993)

6. [`validateMarketController(address,Id,address)`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L449)
   - [x] positive: accepts the deployed controller before and after market creation — [`test_validateMarketController_acceptsExactRoleSets`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L283), [`test_validateMarketController_acceptsCanonicalControllerForCreatedMarket`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L287)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a controller bound to another core (`LidoLendMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_rejectsControllerBoundToAnotherCore`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L293)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a non-canonical controller (`RegisteredControllerMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_rejectsNonCanonicalControllerForCreatedMarket`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L305)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a market id mismatch (`MarketIdMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_rejectsMarketIdMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L330)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a proxy admin mismatch (`ProxyAdminMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_rejectsProxyAdminMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L317)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a registry mismatch (`ControllerGuardianMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_rejectsGuardianMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L341)
   - [x] negative: an extra holder of any known role reverts (`RoleMemberCountMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_unexpectedHolderOfEveryKnownRole`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L352)
   - [x] negative: a substituted manager, pauser, admin, freezer or unfreezer reverts (`MissingRole`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_rejectsManagerSubstitution`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L390), [`testRevert_validateMarketController_rejectsPauserSubstitution`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L407), [`testRevert_validateMarketController_rejectsAdminFreezerAndUnfreezerSubstitution`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L424)
   - [x] negative: a substituted `RESUMER_ROLE` holder reverts (`MissingRole`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_rejectsResumerSubstitution`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L1002)
   - [x] negative: a known role with a non-default admin reverts (`RoleAdminMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_unexpectedAdminOfEveryKnownRole`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L458)
   - [x] negative: the factory still holding `DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE` reverts (`UnexpectedRole`) — [`testRevert_validateMarketController_factoryStillAdministers`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L492)

7. [`validateERC4626Wrapper(address,address,MarketParams)`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L460)
   - [x] positive: accepts the deployed wrapper — [`test_validateERC4626Wrapper_acceptsExpectedBinding`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L752)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a wrapper bound to another core (`LidoLendMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateERC4626Wrapper_rejectsWrapperBoundToAnotherCore`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L756)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a cached market id or params mismatch (`MarketIdMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateERC4626Wrapper_rejectsCachedMarketIdMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L783), [`testRevert_validateERC4626Wrapper_rejectsCachedMarketParamsMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L768)

   - [x] negative: reverts on a proxy admin mismatch (`ProxyAdminMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateERC4626Wrapper_rejectsProxyAdminMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L808)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a controller mismatch (`ERC4626WrapperControllerMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateERC4626Wrapper_rejectsControllerMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L821)
   - [x] negative: reverts when the supplied controller fails the core or canonical binding — [`testRevert_validateERC4626Wrapper_suppliedControllerFailsBinding`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L1018)

8. [`validateGuardianSignals(address,GuardianSpec)`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L474)
   - [x] positive: accepts the deployed registry — [`test_validateGuardianSignals_acceptsExactRoleSets`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L528)
   - [x] positive: accepts a compatible proxy with another runtime codehash — [`test_validateGuardianSignals_acceptsCompatibleProxyWithDifferentRuntimeCodehash`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L535)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a proxy admin mismatch (`ProxyAdminMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_rejectsProxyAdminMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L558)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a quorum or suspect window mismatch — [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_rejectsQuorumMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L571), [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_rejectsSuspectWindowMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L579)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a guardian count mismatch or a missing guardian — [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_rejectsGuardianCountMismatch`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L589), [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_rejectsMissingGuardian`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L598)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a duplicate expected guardian (`DuplicateGuardian`) — [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_rejectsDuplicateExpectedGuardian`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L607)
   - [x] negative: an extra holder of any known role reverts (`RoleMemberCountMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_unexpectedHolderOfEveryKnownRole`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L623), [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_unexpectedAccessLimiter`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L704)
   - [x] negative: a substituted manager, restorer or admin reverts (`MissingRole`) — [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_rejectsGovernanceRoleSubstitution`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L657), [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_rejectsDefaultAdminSubstitution`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L684)
   - [x] negative: a known role with a non-default admin reverts (`RoleAdminMismatch`) — [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_unexpectedAdminOfEveryKnownRole`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L722)
   - [x] negative: the factory still holding `DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE` reverts (`UnexpectedRole`) — [`testRevert_validateGuardianSignals_factoryStillAdministers`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L1040)
   - [-] negative: the factory still holding `MANAGER_ROLE` reverts (cannot happen: the sole manager must be `LIDO_LEND_OWNER`, and the constructor's core-binding call to it would fail for the factory's own address, which has no code yet)

9. views: [`CONTROLLER_IMPL`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L291), [`ERC4626_WRAPPER_IMPL`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L293), [`GUARDIAN_SIGNALS_IMPL`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L295), [`LIDO_LEND`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L298), [`IRM_ROUTER`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L300), [`CIRCUIT_BREAKER`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L302), [`RESEAL_MANAGER`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L304), [`LIDO_LEND_OWNER`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L306), [`RISK_STEWARD_DASHBOARD`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L308), [`ADMIN`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L312)
   - [x] positive: deployed proxies use the factory's implementations and admin — [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol): [`test_deployMarketComponents_producesInertWiredComponents`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L99), [`test_deployGuardianSignals_producesWiredRegistry`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol#L476)
   - [x] positive: every immutable reads back its constructor argument — [`test_views_readBackConstructorArguments`](test/unit/LidoLendMarketFactory.t.sol#L1062)

5. [`GuardianSignalsDeployed(address)`](src/periphery/LidoLendMarketFactory.sol#L274)

### `LidoLendOwner` — [`src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol), [`test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol), [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol)

1. [`constructor(ILidoLend,address,address,address)`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol#L52)
   - [x] positive: grants admin, creator and fee manager; zero holders get nothing — [`test_constructor_grantsProvidedRoleHolders`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L714)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero core or zero admin (`ZeroAddress`) — [`testRevert_constructor_zeroCore`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L702), [`testRevert_constructor_zeroAdmin`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L708)
   - [x] state effects: the wired facade owns the core — [`test_facade_holdsCoreOwnership`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L200)

2. [`createMarket(MarketParams)`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol#L65)
   - [x] positive: creates a controller-less market; core emits its creation events — [`test_createMarket_withRole_createsMarket`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L211)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MARKET_CREATOR_ROLE` or with a foreign role — [`testRevert_createMarket_withoutRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L230), [`testRevert_createMarket_wrongRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L249)
   - [x] access control: a revoked creator loses access — [`testRevert_createMarket_afterRevoke`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L685)
   - [x] negative: the core rejects it once ownership has moved — [`test_transferCoreOwnership_revokesFacadeControl`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L669)

3. [`createMarket(MarketParams,address,bool)`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol#L70)
   - [x] positive: binds the controller and enables settlement on the core — [`test_createMarket_controlled_bindsControllerAndSettlement`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L259)
   - [x] positive: `enableSettlement = false` leaves settlement disabled — [`test_createMarket_controlled_settlementDisabled`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L282)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MARKET_CREATOR_ROLE` — [`testRevert_createMarketControlled_withoutRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L239)

4. [`setFee(MarketParams,uint256)`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol#L82)
   - [x] positive: sets the market fee; core emits `SetFee` — [`test_setFee_withRole_setsFee`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L554)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `FEE_MANAGER_ROLE` or with a foreign role — [`testRevert_setFee_withoutRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L573), [`testRevert_setFee_wrongRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L586)

5. [`setFeeRecipient(address)`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol#L87)
   - [x] positive: sets the recipient; core emits `SetFeeRecipient` — [`test_setFeeRecipient_withRole_setsRecipient`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L599)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `FEE_MANAGER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_setFeeRecipient_withoutRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L626)

6. [`setMarketSupplyCap(Id,uint256)`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol#L96)
   - [x] positive: reaches the controller the core maps to each id — [`test_marketManagement_dispatchesByMarketId`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L304); [`test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol): [`test_governanceFacade_dispatchesControllerActionsAcrossRealMarkets`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol#L57)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MARKET_MANAGER_ROLE`, also for a guardian manager — [`testRevert_marketManagement_withoutRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L363), [`testRevert_marketAndGuardianManagement_wrongRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L478)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a market with no controller (`UnknownMarket`) — [`testRevert_unknownMarket`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L497)

7. [`unfreezeMarket(Id)`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol#L101)
   - [x] positive: unfreezes the controller the core maps to each id — [`test_marketManagement_dispatchesByMarketId`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L304); [`test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol): [`test_governanceFacade_dispatchesControllerActionsAcrossRealMarkets`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol#L57)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MARKET_MANAGER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_marketManagement_withoutRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L363)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a market with no controller (`UnknownMarket`) — [`testRevert_unknownMarket_everyOtherMarketLever`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L523)

8. [`setMarketCollateralActivationConfig(Id,CollateralActivationConfig)`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol#L106)
   - [x] positive: forwards each market's config to its own controller — [`test_marketManagement_dispatchesByMarketId`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L304); [`test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol): [`test_governanceFacade_dispatchesControllerActionsAcrossRealMarkets`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol#L57)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MARKET_MANAGER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_marketManagement_withoutRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L363)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a market with no controller (`UnknownMarket`) — [`testRevert_unknownMarket_everyOtherMarketLever`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L523)

9. [`setMarketSettlementConfig(Id,SettlementConfig)`](src/periphery/LidoLendOwner.sol#L114)
   - [x] positive: forwards each market's config to its own controller — [`test_marketManagement_dispatchesByMarketId`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L304); [`test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol): [`test_governanceFacade_dispatchesControllerActionsAcrossRealMarkets`](test/scenario/LidoLendOwnerGovernance.t.sol#L57)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `MARKET_MANAGER_ROLE` — [`testRevert_marketManagement_withoutRole`](test/unit/LidoLendOwner.t.sol#L363)

### `RiskStewardDashboard` — [`src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol), [`test/scenario/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol`](test/scenario/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol), [`test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol`](test/scenario/MarketFactory.t.sol)

1. [`constructor(address,ILidoLend)`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L59)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero admin (`ZeroAddress`) — [`test_constructor_zeroAdminReverts`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L426)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero core (`ZeroAddress`) — [`test_constructor_zeroCoreReverts`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L433)
   - [x] positive: only `admin` holds `DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE` — [`test_constructor_onlyAdminHoldsAdminRole`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L110)

2. [`addMarketSteward(Id,address)`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L70)
   - [x] positive: appoints, enumerates and unlocks the levers — [`test_addMarketSteward_appointsAndEnumerates`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L125)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero steward (`ZeroSteward`) — [`test_addMarketSteward_zeroReverts`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L386)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `STEWARD_ADMIN_ROLE` — [`testRevert_addMarketSteward_notAdmin`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L179)
   - [x] boundaries: re-adding an existing steward emits nothing — [`test_addMarketSteward_reAddEmitsNothing`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L393)
   - [x] state effects: an appointment covers only its market id — [`test_marketStewards_isolatedPerMarket`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L173), [`testRevert_steward_cannotActOnUnassignedMarket`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L264)

3. [`removeMarketSteward(Id,address)`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L76)
   - [x] positive: drops the steward and revokes the levers — [`test_removeMarketSteward_dropsFromSet`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L153), [`test_removeMarketSteward_actualRemoveEmits`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L406)
   - [x] access control: reverts without `STEWARD_ADMIN_ROLE` — [`testRevert_removeMarketSteward_notAdmin`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L187)
   - [x] boundaries: removing a non-steward emits nothing — [`test_removeMarketSteward_nonStewardEmitsNothing`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L400)

4. [`increaseActivationDelays(Id,uint40,uint40,uint40)`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L95)
   - [x] positive: raises all three delays and keeps other fields — [`test_increaseActivationDelays_raisesAll`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L200); [`test/scenario/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol`](test/scenario/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol): [`test_increaseActivationDelays_raisesQuorumFallbackAtomically`](test/scenario/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L42)
   - [x] negative: lowering floor, cap or quorum fallback reverts (`NotTightened`) — [`testRevert_increaseActivationDelays_loweringFloor`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L224), [`testRevert_increaseActivationDelays_loweringCap`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L231), [`testRevert_increaseActivationDelays_loweringQuorumFallback`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L238)
   - [x] negative: the controller's own config validation still applies — [`test/scenario/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol`](test/scenario/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol): [`testRevert_increaseActivationDelays_quorumFallbackMustExceedCap`](test/scenario/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L54)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a market with no controller (`UnknownMarket`) — [`testRevert_unknownMarket_hasNoController`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L273)
   - [x] access control: reverts for a non-steward or another market's steward — [`testRevert_increaseActivationDelays_notSteward`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L257), [`testRevert_steward_cannotActOnUnassignedMarket`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L264)
   - [x] boundaries: equal values hold and pass — [`test_increaseActivationDelays_holdIsAllowed`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L214)
   - [x] boundaries: enabling the quorum fallback from zero passes — [`test_increaseActivationDelays_enablesQuorumFallback`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L245)

5. [`increaseActivatingLockDuration(Id,uint40)`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L119)
   - [x] positive: raises the duration and keeps other fields — [`test_increaseActivatingLockDuration_raises`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L299)
   - [x] negative: lowering reverts (`NotTightened`) — [`testRevert_increaseActivatingLockDuration_lowering`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L311)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a market with no controller (`UnknownMarket`) — [`testRevert_unknownMarket_everyOtherConfigLever`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L281)
   - [x] access control: reverts for a non-steward (`NotMarketSteward`) — [`testRevert_increaseActivatingLockDuration_notSteward`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L323)
   - [x] boundaries: an equal duration is accepted — [`test_increaseActivatingLockDuration_equalDuration_accepted`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L317)

6. [`increaseMinDepositAmount(Id,uint128)`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L131)
   - [x] positive: raises the deposit floor and keeps other fields — [`test_increaseMinDepositAmount_raises`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L333)
   - [x] negative: lowering by one wei reverts (`NotTightened`) — [`testRevert_increaseMinDepositAmount_lowering`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L353)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a market with no controller (`UnknownMarket`) — [`testRevert_unknownMarket_everyOtherConfigLever`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L281)
   - [x] access control: reverts for a non-steward (`NotMarketSteward`) — [`testRevert_increaseMinDepositAmount_notSteward`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L362)

7. [`revokeInstantActivation(Id,address)`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L143)
   - [x] positive: forwards the revoke to the controller — [`test_revokeInstantActivation_forwards`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L373), [`test_revokeInstantActivation_emitsEvent`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L414)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a market with no controller (`UnknownMarket`) — [`testRevert_unknownMarket_everyOtherConfigLever`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L281)
   - [x] access control: reverts for a non-steward (`NotMarketSteward`) — [`testRevert_revokeInstantActivation_notSteward`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L381)

8. [`freezeMarket(Id)`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L149)
   - [x] positive: forwards the freeze to the controller — [`test_freezeMarket_forwards`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L442)
   - [x] negative: reverts for a market with no controller (`UnknownMarket`) — [`testRevert_freezeMarket_unknownMarket`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L453)
   - [x] access control: reverts for a non-steward (`NotMarketSteward`) — [`testRevert_freezeMarket_notSteward`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L448)

9. views: [`marketStewards`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L81), [`isMarketSteward`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L86), [`LIDO_LEND`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L34), [`STEWARD_ADMIN_ROLE`](src/periphery/RiskStewardDashboard.sol#L27)
   - [x] positive: `marketStewards` and `isMarketSteward` reflect appointments in order — [`test_addMarketSteward_appointsAndEnumerates`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L125), [`test_marketStewards_isolatedPerMarket`](test/unit/RiskStewardDashboard.t.sol#L173)

### `OssifiableProxy` — [`src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol), [`test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol), [`test/unit/UsdyStableOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/UsdyStableOracle.t.sol)

1. [`constructor(address,address,bytes)`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol#L33)
   - [x] positive: non-empty init data is delegatecalled into the implementation — [`test_constructor_runsInitData`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L67)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a non-contract implementation (`ERC1967InvalidImplementation`) — [`test_constructor_revertsOnNonContractImplementation`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L87)
   - [x] negative: a reverting init call bubbles its error — [`test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol): [`testRevert_initialize_zeroAdmin`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L1460); [`test/unit/UsdyStableOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/UsdyStableOracle.t.sol): [`testRevert_initialize_zeroStableFeed`](test/unit/UsdyStableOracle.t.sol#L175)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero admin (`ERC1967InvalidAdmin`) — [`testRevert_constructor_zeroAdmin`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L92)
   - [x] boundaries: empty init data skips the init call — [`test_constructor_emptyData_skipsInit`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L81)

2. [`proxy__changeAdmin(address)`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol#L66)
   - [x] positive: rotates the admin — [`test_changeAdmin`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L100)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a zero admin (`ERC1967InvalidAdmin`) — [`test_changeAdmin_zeroReverts`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L129)
   - [x] negative: reverts after ossification (`ProxyIsOssified`) — [`test_afterOssify_adminCallsRevertOssified`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L224)
   - [x] access control: reverts for a non-admin (`NotAdmin`) — [`test_changeAdmin_onlyAdmin`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L109)
   - [x] state effects: the previous admin loses access — [`test_changeAdmin_previousAdminLosesAccess`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L115)

3. [`proxy__upgradeTo(address)`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol#L73)
   - [x] positive: swaps the implementation — [`test_upgradeTo`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L138)
   - [x] negative: reverts after ossification (`ProxyIsOssified`) — [`testRevert_afterOssify_ossifyAndUpgradesRevert`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L234)
   - [x] negative: reverts on a non-contract implementation (`ERC1967InvalidImplementation`) — [`testRevert_upgradeTo_nonContractImplementation`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L147)
   - [x] access control: reverts for a non-admin (`NotAdmin`) — [`test_upgradeTo_onlyAdmin`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L154)

4. [`proxy__upgradeToAndCall(address,bytes)`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol#L82)
   - [x] positive: swaps the implementation and runs the setup call — [`test_upgradeToAndCall_withSetupData`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L164)
   - [x] negative: reverts after ossification (`ProxyIsOssified`) — [`testRevert_afterOssify_ossifyAndUpgradesRevert`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L234)
   - [x] negative: a reverting setup call reverts the upgrade — [`testRevert_upgradeToAndCall_revertingSetup_keepsImplementation`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L182)
   - [x] access control: reverts for a non-admin (`NotAdmin`) — [`test_upgradeToAndCall_onlyAdmin`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L193)
   - [x] boundaries: empty setup data skips the call — [`test_upgradeToAndCall_emptyData_skipsCall`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L175)

5. [`proxy__ossify()`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol#L56)
   - [x] positive: zeroes the admin and reports ossified — [`test_ossify`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L203)
   - [x] access control: reverts for a non-admin (`NotAdmin`) — [`test_ossify_onlyAdmin`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L217)
   - [x] boundaries: a second ossify reverts (`ProxyIsOssified`) — [`testRevert_afterOssify_ossifyAndUpgradesRevert`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L234)
   - [x] state effects: calls still delegate to the implementation afterwards — [`test_afterOssify_callsStillDelegate`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L252)

6. [`receive()`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol#L49) / `fallback()`
   - [x] positive: a plain value transfer delegates and the proxy keeps the ether — [`test_receive_delegatesAndHoldsEther`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L267)
   - [x] positive: calls with data reach the current implementation — [`test_constructor_runsInitData`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L67), [`test_upgradeToAndCall_withSetupData`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L164)

7. views: [`proxy__getAdmin`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol#L88), [`proxy__getImplementation`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol#L94), [`proxy__getIsOssified`](src/proxy/OssifiableProxy.sol#L100)
   - [x] positive: report admin, implementation and ossified state — [`test_getters_initialState`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L57), [`test_ossify`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L203)

#### Events
1. `AdminChanged(address,address)`
   - [x] emitted on rotation and on ossify to zero — [`test_changeAdmin`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L100), [`test_ossify`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L203)
   - [x] emitted at construction — [`test_constructor_emitsUpgradedThenAdminChanged`](test/unit/OssifiableProxy.t.sol#L73)
2. `Upgraded(address)`
