### `TrailingAnchorOracleBase` — [`src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol) (shared suite, once through each adapter), [`test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol), [`test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol), [`test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol), [`test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol), [`test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol), [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol)

1. [`constructor(address,address)`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L245)
   - [x] negative: a zero loan or collateral token reverts `ZeroAddress` — [`testRevert_constructor_zeroLoanToken`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L92); [`test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol): [`testRevert_constructor_zeroAddresses`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L28); [`test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol): [`testRevert_constructor_zeroAddresses`](test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol#L45)
   - [x] negative: `initialize` on the implementation reverts `InvalidInitialization` — [`testRevert_constructor_disablesInitializers`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L97)
   - [x] positive: `PRICE_SCALE` is `10^(36 + loanDecimals − collateralDecimals − 18)` — [`test_price_scalesCurrentPrice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L226); [`test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol): [`testFuzz_constructor_priceScale`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L49); [`test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol): [`testFuzz_price_scalesToShareDecimals`](test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol#L140)
   - [x] negative: decimals that make that exponent negative revert `UnsupportedDecimals(loanDecimals, collateralDecimals)` — [`test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol): [`testFuzz_constructor_priceScale`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L49)

2. [`initialize(address,Parameters,uint256,uint256)`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L266)
   - [x] positive: seeds `anchorPrice` and `basePrice` at the current price and stores the parameters, with no suspension or pending anchor; `price()` returns at once — [`test_initialize_seedsAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L103)
   - [x] state effects: emits `ParametersSet`, then `AnchorSet(price, now, true)` — [`test_initialize_seedsAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L103)
   - [x] state effects: grants `DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE` to `admin` alone; `MANAGER_ROLE` and `PRICE_ACCEPTOR_ROLE` start empty — [`test_initialize_seedsAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L103)
   - [x] negative: a zero `admin` reverts `ZeroAddress` — [`testRevert_initialize_zeroAdmin`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L129)
   - [x] negative: a second call reverts `InvalidInitialization` — [`testRevert_initialize_twice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L135)
   - [x] negative: a zero current price reverts `ZeroPrice`; a source revert propagates, a stale vault price included — [`testRevert_initialize_sourceFails`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L140); [`test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol): [`testRevert_seedAndAcceptance_stalePrice`](test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol#L104)
   - [x] negative: a current price outside `[minPrice, maxPrice]` reverts `UnexpectedPrice(currentPrice, minPrice, maxPrice)` — [`testRevert_initialize_seedOutsideRange`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L176)
   - [x] negative: each out-of-range parameter reverts with the error `setParameters` gives — [`testRevert_initialize_invalidParameters`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L198)
   - [x] boundaries: a current price on either edge of the range is seeded — [`test_initialize_seedRangeEdgesInclusive`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L153)

3. [`price()`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L283)
   - [x] positive: returns the current price times `PRICE_SCALE`: 1.15e18 is 1.15e24 at 6 loan and 18 collateral decimals — [`test_price_scalesCurrentPrice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L226); [`test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol): [`test_price_usdcExample`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L37)
   - [x] positive: any current price, suspension and time give the scaled price or the error of the first failed step — [`testFuzz_price_matchesRule`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L295); [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_priceMatchesRule`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L138)
   - [x] negative: while suspended it reverts `PricingSuspended(pricingResumesAt)`, ahead of every other check — [`test_setPricingResumesAt_suspendsUntilTheTime`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1259), [`testFuzz_price_matchesRule`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L295); [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_priceMatchesRule`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L138)
   - [x] negative: a source revert propagates; a zero current price reverts `ZeroPrice` — [`testRevert_price_sourceFails`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L266)
   - [x] negative: a price outside the bounds reverts `PriceOutOfBounds(currentPrice, lowerBound, upperBound)` — [`test_price_boundsEdgesInclusive`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L233), [`test_price_boundsAreSeparate`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L250)
   - [x] negative: an open pending anchor lets no price through before an update re-anchors — [`test_price_ignoresPendingAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L277)
   - [x] boundaries: both bounds, rounded down, are inclusive; one wei past either reverts — [`test_price_boundsEdgesInclusive`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L233)
   - [x] boundaries: the two bounds are separate: 0.08% passes below the anchor and fails above it — [`test_price_boundsAreSeparate`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L250)
   - [ ] boundaries: `price()` overflows only for a current price above `type(uint256).max / PRICE_SCALE`
   - [x] positive: reads only the first two slots of the namespace, also with a pending anchor and a past suspension stored — [`test_price_readsTwoSlots`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L308)
   - [x] negative: a bounds revert or a suspension stops only the market's priced paths — [`test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol): [`test_boundsRevert_stopsPricedPathsOnly`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol#L308), [`test_suspension_stopsPricedPathsOnly`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol#L317)

4. [`updateAnchor()`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L299)
   - [x] positive: returns the outcome of each case, which `AnchorUpdated` carries: `WithinBounds`, `PendingAnchorRecorded`, `PendingAnchorHeld`, `PendingAnchorApplied`, `AnchorOnFloor`, `AnchorOnCeiling` — [`test_updateAnchor_returnsTheOutcomeOfEachCase`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L549)
   - [x] positive: any price and time, with or without a pending anchor, give the model's state, outcome and events, `AnchorUpdated` last — [`testFuzz_updateAnchor_matchesModel`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L809); [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_storedStateMatchesModel`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L100)
   - [x] access control: any caller can update — [`test_updateAnchor_takesPriceInsideReach`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L335)
   - [x] positive: a price within the bounds and the rates' reach becomes the anchor — [`test_updateAnchor_takesPriceInsideReach`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L335)
   - [x] positive: a price on a bound moves the anchor by the rate compounded over the time since it was set, rounded down — [`test_updateAnchor_risesAtRiseRate`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L347), [`test_updateAnchor_fallsAtFallRate`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L451)
   - [x] boundaries: a year of drift is the same for one update and for daily ones, and never passes the continuous rate — [`test_updateAnchor_yearOfRiseIndependentOfSchedule`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L358), [`test_updateAnchor_yearOfFallIndependentOfSchedule`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L462)
   - [x] boundaries: any update schedule over a year stays between one whole-year step and the continuous rate — [`testFuzz_updateAnchor_yearOfRiseNeverPassesTheContinuousRate`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L386), [`testFuzz_updateAnchor_yearOfFallNeverPassesTheContinuousRate`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L488)
   - [x] boundaries: after a long rest, at the highest fall rate too, a drift stops on the price — [`test_updateAnchor_longRestMovesOnlyToThePrice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L505), [`test_updateAnchor_highestFallRateAfterLongRest`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L514)
   - [x] boundaries: a second update in the block of a move moves nothing — [`test_updateAnchor_sameBlockDoesNotCompound`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L527); [`test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol): [`test_creepingSource_gainsBoundOnceThenRiseRate`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L188)
   - [x] positive: a price on the anchor with no pending anchor changes nothing and does not revert — [`test_updateAnchor_nothingToDo`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L539)
   - [x] negative: a price outside the bounds never drifts the anchor, however long it rested — [`test_updateAnchor_noDriftOutsideBounds`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L577)
   - [x] negative: a source revert propagates and a zero price reverts `ZeroPrice`, before any change — [`testRevert_updateAnchor_sourceFails`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L588)
   - [x] negative: a price above the `uint104` range reverts `SafeCastOverflowedUintDowncast` — [`testRevert_priceAboveTheStoredRange`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1807)
   - [x] state effects: a price outside the bounds becomes the pending anchor at the update's time, with `PendingAnchorSet`; the anchor stays — [`test_updateAnchor_recordsThePriceAsPendingAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L604), [`test_updateAnchor_noDriftOutsideBounds`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L577)
   - [x] state effects: during the delay, updates on the pending anchor's side change nothing, even after a further move — [`test_updateAnchor_holdsWhilePendingAnchorWaits`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L617)
   - [x] boundaries: a re-anchor runs from `reanchorDelay` after the record through the window's last second, not a second before — [`test_updateAnchor_reanchorsWhenTheWindowOpens`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L629), [`test_updateAnchor_reanchorsOnTheWindowsLastSecond`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L648), [`test_updateAnchor_holdsWhilePendingAnchorWaits`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L617)
   - [x] boundaries: one second after the window the pending anchor has expired and the price is recorded anew — [`test_updateAnchor_expiredPendingAnchorRecordedAnew`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L659)
   - [x] positive: a re-anchor moves onto the nearer of the pending anchor and the current price — [`test_updateAnchor_fallShortHoldsAndReanchorsOnThePrice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L715), [`test_updateAnchor_riseShortHoldsAndReanchorsOnThePrice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L734), [`test_updateAnchor_reanchorRecordsAFurtherMove`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L673), [`test_updateAnchor_dailyAccrualAfterAFallReanchors`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L752)
   - [x] positive: a fall or rise past the bounds is priced one delay after its record — [`test_updateAnchor_largeFallPricedAfterOneDelay`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L695), [`test_updateAnchor_largeRisePricedAfterOneDelay`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L705)
   - [x] state effects: a re-anchor emits `AnchorSet(target, now, false)`, then `PendingAnchorCleared`, or `PendingAnchorSet` for a price still outside, then `AnchorUpdated` — [`test_updateAnchor_reanchorsWhenTheWindowOpens`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L629), [`test_updateAnchor_reanchorRecordsAFurtherMove`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L673)
   - [x] state effects: a price back within the bounds drops the pending anchor with `PendingAnchorCleared` — [`test_updateAnchor_backInBoundsDropsPendingAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L768)
   - [x] state effects: a price outside on the other side replaces the pending anchor — [`test_updateAnchor_otherSideReplacesPendingAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L781)
   - [x] boundaries: a delay and a window at the `uint40` maximum do not overflow — [`test_updateAnchor_hugeDelayDoesNotOverflow`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L793)
   - [x] boundaries: a re-anchor down stops on the floor and one up on the ceiling; the price is recorded again and `basePrice` stays — [`test_updateAnchor_reanchorDownStopsAtTheFloor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L878), [`test_updateAnchor_reanchorUpStopsAtTheCeiling`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1019)
   - [x] boundaries: a source past the bound in every window walks the anchor onto the floor or the ceiling and no further — [`test_updateAnchor_walkStopsAtTheFloor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L918), [`test_updateAnchor_walkStopsAtTheCeiling`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1060)
   - [x] boundaries: a drift stops on the floor too — [`test_updateAnchor_driftStopsAtTheFloor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L942)
   - [x] state effects: on its line, an update in the window changes nothing (`AnchorOnFloor`, `AnchorOnCeiling`); after expiry the price is recorded again — [`test_updateAnchor_pinnedOnTheFloorChangesNothing`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L899), [`test_updateAnchor_pinnedOnTheCeilingChangesNothing`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1040)
   - [x] boundaries: a zero `maxAnchorBelowBase` stops every fall and a zero `maxAnchorAboveBase` every re-anchor over `basePrice`; drifts up still run — [`test_updateAnchor_zeroBelowBaseStopsEveryOpenFall`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L997), [`test_updateAnchor_zeroAboveBaseStopsReanchorsOverTheBasePrice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1086)
   - [ ] boundaries: a zero `maxAnchorContinuousRiseRate` or `maxAnchorContinuousFallRate` stops the drift on its side
   - [x] state effects: `basePrice` follows a drift up, stays through a re-anchor, rises by a later drift's step only, and stays through falls — [`test_updateAnchor_basePriceFollowsDriftsOnly`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L958); [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_basePriceFollowsItsRule`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L180)
   - [x] positive: any walk moves the anchor only by a drift inside the rates' reach or a waited re-anchor onto the nearer target, never under the floor or over the ceiling — [`testFuzz_updateAnchor_movesOnlyByReachOrWaitedPendingAnchors`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L827); [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_anchorMovesOnlyByReachOrWaitedSteps`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L161), [`invariant_anchorNeverUnderTheFloor`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L167), [`invariant_anchorNeverOverTheCeiling`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L173)
   - [x] state effects: never stops or changes a price `price()` returned just before it — [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_updateKeepsReturnedPrice`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L153)
   - [x] state effects: runs during a pricing suspension — [`test_setPricingResumesAt_updatesRunDuringSuspension`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1296)
   - [x] state effects: an update with nothing to move writes no slot; a drift reads the first three slots and writes the first only — [`test_updateAnchor_noMoveWritesNothing`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1504), [`test_updateAnchor_driftReadsThreeSlots`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1531)

5. [`acceptPrice(uint256,uint256)`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L325)
   - [x] positive: sets the anchor and `basePrice` to the current price at any distance and drops the pending anchor; `price()` returns it at once — [`test_acceptPrice_anyDistanceDropsPendingAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1127); [`test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol): [`test_acceptance_restartsAnchor`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol#L329)
   - [x] state effects: emits `AnchorSet(price, now, true)`, then `PendingAnchorCleared` — [`test_acceptPrice_anyDistanceDropsPendingAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1127)
   - [x] positive: moves the anchor past the floor or the ceiling, and the next line hangs off the accepted price — [`test_acceptPrice_underTheFloorRestartsTheBasePrice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1145), [`test_acceptPrice_overTheCeilingRestartsTheBasePrice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1167)
   - [x] negative: a current price outside the range reverts `UnexpectedPrice(currentPrice, minPrice, maxPrice)` — [`test_acceptPrice_rangeEdgesInclusive`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1188); [`test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol): [`test_acceptance_refusesPriceMovedAfterCheck`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol#L355)
   - [x] negative: a zero current price reverts `ZeroPrice`, inside the range or out of it; a source revert propagates — [`testRevert_acceptPrice_sourceFails`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1226); [`test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol): [`testRevert_seedAndAcceptance_stalePrice`](test/unit/CentrifugeNavOracle.t.sol#L104)
   - [x] negative: a price above the `uint104` range reverts `SafeCastOverflowedUintDowncast` — [`testRevert_priceAboveTheStoredRange`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1807)
   - [x] access control: anyone, the admin and the manager revert `AccessControlUnauthorizedAccount` — [`testRevert_acceptPrice_notPriceAcceptor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1113)
   - [x] boundaries: both edges of the range are accepted — [`test_acceptPrice_rangeEdgesInclusive`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1188)
   - [x] boundaries: runs in the seed's block and during a suspension — [`test_acceptPrice_inTheSeedsBlockDuringSuspension`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1216)

6. [`setPricingResumesAt(uint40)`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L333)
   - [x] positive: `price()` reverts `PricingSuspended(resumesAt)` until `resumesAt` and returns from that second — [`test_setPricingResumesAt_suspendsUntilTheTime`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1259)
   - [x] state effects: stores the time and emits `PricingResumesAtSet(resumesAt)` — [`test_setPricingResumesAt_suspendsUntilTheTime`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1259), [`test_setPricingResumesAt_capEdge`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1274)
   - [x] negative: a time more than `MAX_PRICING_SUSPENSION` after the call reverts `InvalidPricingResumesAt(resumesAt)` — [`test_setPricingResumesAt_capEdge`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1274)
   - [x] boundaries: exactly `MAX_PRICING_SUSPENSION` after the call is accepted — [`test_setPricingResumesAt_capEdge`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1274)
   - [x] boundaries: a time not in the future lifts a suspension at once — [`test_setPricingResumesAt_pastLifts`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1287)
   - [x] state effects: updates and acceptances still run during a suspension — [`test_setPricingResumesAt_updatesRunDuringSuspension`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1296), [`test_acceptPrice_inTheSeedsBlockDuringSuspension`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1216)
   - [x] state effects: a suspension never ends more than `MAX_PRICING_SUSPENSION` after the call that set it — [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_suspensionWithinCap`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L196)
   - [x] access control: anyone, the admin and the price acceptor revert `AccessControlUnauthorizedAccount` — [`testRevert_setPricingResumesAt_notManager`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1246)

7. [`setParameters(Parameters)`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L346)
   - [x] positive: stores all eight parameters and drops the pending anchor; the anchor and `basePrice` stay — [`test_setParameters_storesAndDropsPendingAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1327)
   - [x] state effects: emits `ParametersSet`, then `PendingAnchorCleared` — [`test_setParameters_storesAndDropsPendingAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1327)
   - [ ] state effects: `setParameters` with unchanged values still emits `ParametersSet` and drops the pending anchor
   - [x] negative: a zero bound reverts `MaxPriceAboveAnchorIsZero` or `MaxPriceBelowAnchorIsZero` — [`test_setParameters_validatorEdges`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1415)
   - [x] negative: a bound of 1e18 reverts `MaxPriceAboveAnchorNotBelowWad` or `MaxPriceBelowAnchorNotBelowWad` — [`test_setParameters_validatorEdges`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1415)
   - [x] negative: a `maxAnchorBelowBase` or `maxAnchorAboveBase` of 1e18 reverts `MaxAnchorBelowBaseNotBelowWad` or `MaxAnchorAboveBaseNotBelowWad` — [`test_setParameters_validatorEdges`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1415)
   - [x] negative: a rate over 1e18 reverts `MaxAnchorContinuousRiseRateExceedsWad` or `MaxAnchorContinuousFallRateExceedsWad` — [`test_setParameters_validatorEdges`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1415)
   - [x] negative: a delay or a window under an hour reverts `ReanchorDelayBelowMinReanchorDelay` or `ReanchorWindowBelowMinReanchorWindow` — [`test_setParameters_validatorEdges`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1415)
   - [x] negative: a delay over which a rate passes its bound reverts `RiseOverDelayExceedsMaxPriceAboveAnchor` or `FallOverDelayExceedsMaxPriceBelowAnchor` — [`test_setParameters_validatorEdges`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1415), [`test_setParameters_riseOverOneDelayEqualToTheBound`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L406), [`test_setParameters_fallRateEdgeOverOneDelay`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L429)
   - [x] boundaries: each limit's last valid value is accepted: one-wei and almost-whole bounds, rates of 1e18, a one-hour delay and window, each rate's longest delay, zero and almost-whole floor and ceiling distances — [`test_setParameters_validatorEdges`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1415), [`test_updateAnchor_highestFallRateAfterLongRest`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L514)
   - [x] boundaries: a rise or fall over one delay exactly equal to its bound is accepted, one wei more reverts — [`test_setParameters_riseOverOneDelayEqualToTheBound`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L406), [`test_setParameters_fallRateEdgeOverOneDelay`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L429)
   - [x] state effects: a narrower bound refuses at once a price the old one took — [`test_setParameters_boundsApplyAtOnce`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1352)
   - [x] state effects: new bounds and rates apply from the next call, a new rate over the whole time since the anchor was set — [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_priceMatchesRule`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L138), [`invariant_storedStateMatchesModel`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L100)
   - [x] state effects: a narrower `maxAnchorBelowBase` or `maxAnchorAboveBase` leaves an anchor already past the new line where it is — [`test_setParameters_narrowerBelowBaseKeepsADeeperAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1365), [`test_setParameters_narrowerAboveBaseKeepsAHigherAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1389)
   - [x] state effects: never changes a price `price()` returned — [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_parameterChangesKeepReturnedPrice`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L191)
   - [x] state effects: the stored parameters always pass the validator — [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_parametersValid`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L105)
   - [x] positive: takes a set that breaks the sizing rule, which no contract checks — [`test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol): [`test_wrongJumpUp_corridorWiderThanTheSizingRuleLeavesBadDebt`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol#L229)
   - [x] access control: anyone, the admin and the price acceptor revert `AccessControlUnauthorizedAccount` — [`testRevert_setParameters_notManager`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1313)

8. views: [`PRICE_SCALE`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L100), [`MIN_REANCHOR_DELAY`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L103), [`MIN_REANCHOR_WINDOW`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L107), [`MAX_PRICING_SUSPENSION`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L109), [`MANAGER_ROLE`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L85), [`PRICE_ACCEPTOR_ROLE`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L87), [`anchorPrice`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L198), [`basePrice`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L202), [`anchorAt`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L204), [`pendingAnchorPrice`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L231), [`pendingAnchorAt`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L215), [`pricingResumesAt`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L213), [`maxPriceAboveAnchor`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L208), [`maxPriceBelowAnchor`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L211), [`maxAnchorContinuousRiseRate`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L219), [`maxAnchorContinuousFallRate`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L222), [`maxAnchorBelowBase`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L225), [`maxAnchorAboveBase`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L228), [`reanchorDelay`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L233), [`reanchorWindow`](src/oracle/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.sol#L236)
   - [x] positive: `MANAGER_ROLE` and `PRICE_ACCEPTOR_ROLE` are the hashes of their names, both administered by `DEFAULT_ADMIN_ROLE` — [`test_roles_idsAndAdmin`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L213)
   - [x] positive: `PRICE_SCALE` reads the scale recomputed from the token decimals — [`test_price_scalesCurrentPrice`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L226)
   - [x] positive: `MAX_PRICING_SUSPENSION` reads 7 days and `MIN_REANCHOR_WINDOW` 1 hour; the delay error carries the 1-hour minimum — [`test_setPricingResumesAt_capEdge`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1274), [`test_setParameters_validatorEdges`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1415)
   - [x] positive: the getters read back the seed, the parameters and the pending anchor — [`test_initialize_seedsAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L103), [`test_updateAnchor_recordsThePriceAsPendingAnchor`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L604)
   - [x] positive: the stored values sit in the adapter's ERC-7201 namespace, four slots in the specified layout — [`test_storage_namespaceLayout`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1470)
   - [x] positive: `anchorPrice` is never zero and `anchorAt` never in the future — [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_anchorNonZeroAndNotInFuture`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L121)
   - [x] positive: a pending anchor's price and time are set together, never in the future, outside the anchor's bounds — [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_pendingAnchorConsistent`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L128)
   - [x] state effects: an upgrade to a new build of the same adapter keeps every stored value and the price — [`test_upgrade_sameAdapterKeepsState`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1854)
   - [ ] state effects: an upgrade keeps the roles and the initializer flag, which live in OpenZeppelin's namespaces

### `UsdyNavOracle` — [`src/oracle/UsdyNavOracle.sol`](src/oracle/UsdyNavOracle.sol)
Tests: [`test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol), [`test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol) (`TrailingAnchorOracleBaseUsdyTest`), [`test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol), [`test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol), [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol) (`TrailingAnchorOracleUsdyInvariant`, `TrailingAnchorOracleSizedInvariant`)

1. [`constructor(address,address,address)`](src/oracle/UsdyNavOracle.sol#L27)
   - [x] negative: a zero source or collateral token reverts `ZeroAddress` — [`testRevert_constructor_zeroAddresses`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L28)
   - [x] positive: stores the getter as `SOURCE` and sets `PRICE_SCALE` from the tokens' decimals, or reverts `UnsupportedDecimals` — [`test_price_usdcExample`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L37), [`testFuzz_constructor_priceScale`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L49)

2. [`_currentPrice()`](src/oracle/UsdyNavOracle.sol#L39) (the source read behind every price, update, acceptance and seed)
   - [x] positive: the current price is the getter's 18-decimal USD value: the anchor keeps it, and `price()` returns it times `PRICE_SCALE` — [`test_price_usdcExample`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L37), [`testFuzz_price_scalesToLoanDecimals`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L71)
   - [x] negative: a getter revert propagates through `price()`, `updateAnchor()`, `acceptPrice` and `initialize` — [`test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol): [`testRevert_price_sourceFails`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L266), [`testRevert_updateAnchor_sourceFails`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L588), [`testRevert_acceptPrice_sourceFails`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L1226), [`testRevert_initialize_sourceFails`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol#L140)
   - [x] positive: reads no source age: a getter frozen at its last value stays priced a month later — [`test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol): [`test_frozenSource_staysPriced`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol#L379)
   - [x] positive: the base's unit suite and invariant campaign run through this adapter — [`test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol); [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol)
   - [x] positive: a year of daily accrual is priced every day under a daily keeper; without one the price leaves the bound on day five — [`test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol): [`test_replay_accruingToken`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L92), [`test_replayWithoutKeeper_leavesBoundOnDayFive`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L162)
   - [x] positive: on a mainnet fork, an oracle over Ondo's live USDY oracle seeds its anchor from its value and returns it times `PRICE_SCALE` — [`test/fork/NavOracleSources.t.sol`](test/fork/NavOracleSources.t.sol): [`test_usdyNavOracle_readsTheOndoOracle`](test/fork/NavOracleSources.t.sol#L72)

3. views: [`SOURCE`](src/oracle/UsdyNavOracle.sol#L20)
   - [x] positive: `SOURCE` reads back the getter — [`test_price_usdcExample`](test/unit/UsdyNavOracle.t.sol#L37)

   - [x] positive: the current price is the feed's answer times `10^(18 − decimals)`, exactly, for 0 to 18 decimals; `price()` returns it times `PRICE_SCALE` — [`test_price_sixDecimalFeed`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L79), [`test_price_eighteenDecimalFeed`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L91), [`testFuzz_price_scalesFromFeedDecimals`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L103)
   - [x] positive: the feed mock returns constant round fields, the stamp as `updatedAt` and the answer at its decimals, rounded down — [`test_feedMock_returnsTheRoundTuple`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L38)
   - [x] negative: a value written more than `MAX_PRICE_AGE` ago reverts `StalePrice(updatedAt)` in `price()` and `updateAnchor()` — [`test_price_maxPriceAgeEdge`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L117)
   - [x] negative: `initialize` and `acceptPrice` refuse a stale value with `StalePrice` — [`testRevert_seedAndAcceptance_stalePrice`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L168)
   - [x] negative: a stamp after the block reverts `PriceFromFuture(updatedAt)`, one past `uint64` included — [`testRevert_price_stampAfterTheBlock`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L131)
   - [x] negative: a negative answer reverts `NegativePrice(answer)`, a stale one `StalePrice` first, and a zero one the base's `ZeroPrice` — [`testRevert_price_negativeOrZeroAnswer`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L148)
   - [x] boundaries: a value exactly `MAX_PRICE_AGE` old, or written at the block time, is returned — [`test_price_maxPriceAgeEdge`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L117), [`testRevert_price_stampAfterTheBlock`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L131)
   - [x] positive: the stamp errors keep `CentrifugeNavOracle`'s signatures, which the shared model encodes — [`test_stampErrors_matchCentrifugeNavOracle`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L186)
   - [x] negative: under random restamps every call refuses a stale or future stamp with the predicted error — [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol): [`invariant_priceMatchesRule`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L138), [`test_stampCases_areReachable`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L310)
   - [x] positive: the base's unit suite and invariant campaign run through this adapter — [`test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorOracleBase.t.sol); [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol)
   - [x] positive: on a mainnet fork, an oracle over each of Chainlink's JTRSY NAV and JAAA NAV feeds seeds its anchor from the feed and returns it times `PRICE_SCALE`, within 0.5% of the token's Centrifuge vault — [`test/fork/NavOracleSources.t.sol`](test/fork/NavOracleSources.t.sol): [`test_chainlinkNavOracle_readsTheChainlinkFeeds`](test/fork/NavOracleSources.t.sol#L121)
   - [x] positive: on a mainnet fork, over each of Chronicle's four adapters a deploy reverts `NotTolled` for the proxy until its predicted address is whitelisted, then seeds the same way — [`test/fork/NavOracleSources.t.sol`](test/fork/NavOracleSources.t.sol): [`test_chainlinkNavOracle_readsTheChronicleAdapters`](test/fork/NavOracleSources.t.sol#L128)

3. views: [`FEED`](src/oracle/ChainlinkNavOracle.sol#L28), [`MAX_PRICE_AGE`](src/oracle/ChainlinkNavOracle.sol#L30), [`ANSWER_SCALE`](src/oracle/ChainlinkNavOracle.sol#L32)
   - [x] positive: read back the constructor arguments and the scale — [`test_price_sixDecimalFeed`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L79), [`test_constructor_maxPriceAge`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L72), [`testFuzz_price_scalesFromFeedDecimals`](test/unit/ChainlinkNavOracle.t.sol#L103)

## Scenario testing
Cross-contract flows on a freshly deployed stack with mock tokens ([`test/setup/LidoLendSetup.sol`](test/setup/LidoLendSetup.sol)); actions go through [`test/setup/MarketActions.sol`](test/setup/MarketActions.sol), which recomputes every expected amount.

- [x] supply, mature collateral, borrow, accrue, repay and withdraw in one run — [`test/scenario/BorrowLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/BorrowLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_fullBorrowLifecycle_roundTrips`](test/scenario/BorrowLifecycle.t.sol#L35)
- [x] every money-flow lever on one declining-price run, cross-step couplings included — [`test/scenario/FullLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/FullLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_fullLifecycle_allActionsLive`](test/scenario/FullLifecycle.t.sol#L104)
- [x] fresh collateral stays locked until it matures; a ban or the quorum delay holds it — [`test/scenario/Activation.t.sol`](test/scenario/Activation.t.sol): [`test_freshCollateral_isLockedUntilMatured`](test/scenario/Activation.t.sol#L86), [`test_bannedBorrower_staysLocked_evenPastTheTimer`](test/scenario/Activation.t.sol#L119), [`test_activateCollateral_waitsOutGuardiansQuorumDelay`](test/scenario/Activation.t.sol#L104)
- [x] the quorum frontier gates maturation; a silent committee's fallback releases it — [`test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol): [`testRevert_TimerElapsedButFrontierBehind_BorrowBlocked`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol#L103), [`test_QuorumOfHeartbeatsPastUnlock_MaturesLot`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol#L129), [`test_SilentCommittee_FallbackDelayMaturesLot`](test/scenario/GuardianLiveness.t.sol#L160)
- [x] a suspect report delays only a lot started before it expires — [`test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol): [`test_staleCount_allReportsLapsed_addsNoDelay`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol#L149), [`test_staleCount_lapsedReportUnderALiveOne_delaysTheLot`](test/scenario/SuspectDelay.t.sol#L198)
- [x] a healthy position is untouchable; a moderate drop leaves no bad debt, a severe one socializes it — [`test/scenario/Liquidation.t.sol`](test/scenario/Liquidation.t.sol): [`test_healthyPosition_cannotBeLiquidated`](test/scenario/Liquidation.t.sol#L167), [`test_liquidate_moderateDrop_noBadDebt`](test/scenario/Liquidation.t.sol#L71), [`test_liquidateBadDebt_severeDrop_socialized`](test/scenario/Liquidation.t.sol#L87)
- [x] gated collateral cannot be seized to a blocked keeper or receiver — [`test/scenario/GatedCollateralMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/GatedCollateralMarket.t.sol): [`testRevert_liquidate_seizeToBlockedKeeper`](test/scenario/GatedCollateralMarket.t.sol#L71), [`testRevert_fallbackExit_seizeToBlockedReceiver`](test/scenario/GatedCollateralMarket.t.sol#L100)
- [x] freeze blocks borrowing; liquidation and settlement still proceed — [`test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol): [`testRevert_FrozenMarket_BorrowBlocked`](test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol#L108), [`test_FrozenMarket_LiquidationStillProceeds`](test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol#L83), [`test_FrozenMarket_SettleStillProceeds`](test/scenario/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol#L54)
- [x] pause rejects every hooked action — [`test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol): [`testRevert_Paused_CoreSupplyRejected`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L82), [`testRevert_Paused_BorrowRejected`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L105), [`testRevert_Paused_RepayRejected`](test/scenario/PauseLifecycle.t.sol#L117)
- [x] settlement policies hold at their exact boundaries, root and non-root — [`test/scenario/DirectSettlementPolicy.t.sol`](test/scenario/DirectSettlementPolicy.t.sol): [`test_nonRootPolicy_boundaries`](test/scenario/DirectSettlementPolicy.t.sol#L83), [`test_rootPolicy_boundaries`](test/scenario/DirectSettlementPolicy.t.sol#L102)
- [x] a fallback exit drains liquidity first; a targeted settle uses only the personal policy — [`test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol): [`test_fallbackExit_drainsLiquidityBeforeApplyingRootPolicy`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol#L220), [`test_settle_actualRootWithLargerQueueQuote_usesOnlyPersonal`](test/scenario/Settlement.t.sol#L279)

- [x] a frozen source stays priced and lent against a month later — [`test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol): [`test_frozenSource_staysPriced`](test/scenario/NavOracleMarket.t.sol#L379)
- [x] a year of accruing-token, treasury-fund and credit-fund NAV, whose sharpest fall sits inside its bound, is priced every day under a daily keeper with no pending anchor — [`test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol): [`test_replay_accruingToken`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L92), [`test_replay_treasuryFund`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L102), [`test_replay_creditFund`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L114), [`test_replay_creditDipInsideBound`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L126)
- [x] a credit-fund drawdown is followed with a 4% floor; with a 1% floor it waits on the floor and then on the ceiling for acceptances — [`test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol): [`test_replay_creditStress_followedWithTheCreditFloor`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L135), [`test_replay_creditStress_waitsOnTheFloorThenTheCeilingAtOnePercent`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L151)
- [x] without a keeper an accruing token leaves its bound on day five; a source held on the upper bound gains the bound once, then only the rise rate — [`test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol): [`test_replayWithoutKeeper_leavesBoundOnDayFive`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L162), [`test_creepingSource_gainsBoundOnceThenRiseRate`](test/scenario/NavOracleReplay.t.sol#L188)
- [ ] negative: an oracle that serves several markets stops all of them at once

[`test/scenario/HeapSettlementBenchmark.t.sol`](test/scenario/HeapSettlementBenchmark.t.sol) only logs gas and asserts no bound.

## Invariant testing
A handler in [`test/invariant/handlers/`](test/invariant/handlers/) drives random bounded actions; every property is checked after each call. `make test-local` runs 1024 runs of depth 32; `fuzz.yml` 4096 runs of depth 64.

### Market solvency — [`test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol)
Actions: supply, withdraw, collateral, borrow, repay, warp, with instant activation.
- [x] total supply assets never fall below total borrow assets — [`invariant_supplyCoversBorrow`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L98)
- [x] the core's loan-token balance covers the idle supply — [`invariant_idleBackedByTokens`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L104)
- [x] the vault forwards every deposit and holds no loan tokens — [`invariant_vaultHoldsNoLoanTokens`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L117)
- [x] the vault owns the whole supply-share book — [`invariant_vaultIsSoleSupplier`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L124)
- [x] the vault's `totalAssets` never exceeds the market's supply — [`invariant_vaultNeverOverReports`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L135)
- [x] the core's collateral balance equals the sum of positions — [`invariant_collateralConservation`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L141)
- [x] no voluntary path leaves debt on active collateral below the floor — [`invariant_noDebtWithSubFloorCollateral`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L156), [`test_borrowBelowFloor_refusedAndPositionRestored`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L290) (the handler reaches the borrow refusal that `SettlementSafety` pins)
- [x] the activation buckets exactly partition each borrower's collateral — [`invariant_unlockTimeSetIffActivating`](test/invariant/MarketSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L172)

### Vault conformance — [`test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol) (18/18), [`test/invariant/VaultConformanceUsdcUsdy.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/VaultConformanceUsdcUsdy.invariant.t.sol) (6/18)
Actions: market actions, price crashes, liquidations, the ERC-4626 surface, targeted settle, fallback exit, personal targets, donations, pause.
- [x] the vault never claims more than the market's accrued supply owes — [`invariant_noValueCreation`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L128)
- [x] share balances sum to the total supply — [`invariant_holdersSumToSupply`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L148)
- [x] donations stay uncounted and never move the price — [`invariant_donationsAreTheOnlyIdleTokens`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L121), [`invariant_donationNeverMovesPrice`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L252)
- [x] `maxWithdraw` and `maxRedeem` fit free liquidity and are zero while paused — [`invariant_maxAreHonouredBounds`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L161)
- [x] `maxDeposit` and `maxMint` are zero when paused and the cap headroom when capped — [`invariant_maxDepositMintReflectState`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L182)
- [x] `withdraw` rounds the burn up, `redeem` rounds the payout down — [`invariant_standardExitRounding`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L209)
- [x] no exit burns more shares than requested — [`invariant_exitBurnBounded`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L257)
- [x] each exit's value lands within its derived rounding bounds — [`invariant_exitPayoutWithinRoundingBounds`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L280)
- [x] a settle burns exactly `ceil(requested * consumed / claim)` shares — [`invariant_settlementRounding`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L225)
- [x] a targeted settle needs a positive personal slice — [`invariant_targetedSettlementRequiresPersonalEligibility`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L232)
- [x] a targeted settle fits the request's pro-rata claim — [`invariant_targetedSettlementStaysWithinGrossClaim`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L240)
- [x] seized collateral ends at the receiver, not in the vault — [`invariant_settlementCollateralGoesToReceiver`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L245)
- [x] seized collateral is worth at most the cancelled debt — [`invariant_settlementRunsAtPar`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L274), [`invariant_settlePayoutWithinCut`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L286)
- [x] the burned supply is worth at least the cancelled debt — [`invariant_settleBurnCoversDebt`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L292)
- [x] the reported `debtCanceled` equals the removed borrow shares' value — [`invariant_settleDebtReportMatchesDeltas`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L298)
- [x] supply falls by exactly the payout plus the cancelled debt — [`invariant_settleConservesAccounting`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L304)
- [x] idle moves only by the liquid payout and final debt rounding — [`invariant_settleLiquidityMatchesDebtRounding`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L263), [`invariant_fallbackExitIdleMatchesRedeem`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L268)
- [x] settlement-budget accounting never underflows — [`invariant_settlementNeverPanics`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L310)
- [x] under bad debt no settle leg goes through — [`invariant_badDebtGateNeverLeaksASettleLeg`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L317)
- [x] a pause stops every settle path, and only a pause raises its error — [`invariant_pauseStopsEverySettlePath`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L381), [`invariant_pauseErrorOnlyOnAPausedMarket`](test/invariant/VaultConformance.invariant.t.sol#L387)

### Exit-book solvency — [`test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol)
Actions: place, fill, cancel on `Erc4626ExitBook` over a mock vault, with yield, liquidity, pause and time moving.
- [x] the book keeps no assets or vault shares — [`invariant_bookHoldsNoAssets`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L119), [`invariant_bookHoldsNoShares`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L124)
- [x] every fill's payouts add up to the filled amount — [`invariant_payoutConservation`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L129)
- [x] request ids only grow — [`invariant_monotonicIds`](test/invariant/ExitBookSolvency.invariant.t.sol#L138)

### Activation inflow — [`test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol)
Actions: credit, voluntary debit and forced seizure over several borrowers in one transaction.
- [x] the inflow accumulator never exceeds its cap — [`invariant_inflowNeverExceedsCap`](test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol#L34)
- [x] without a lock, dust stays below the threshold and the inactive total above it — [`invariant_noLockActivationBucketsRespectThreshold`](test/invariant/ActivationInflow.invariant.t.sol#L44)

### Guardian signals — [`test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol), [`test/invariant/GuardianActivation.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/GuardianActivation.invariant.t.sol)
Actions: every registry mutator over two market ids; then the registry with the controller's unlock policy.
- [x] `isBanned` equals latch or strikes at quorum, both ways — [`invariant_banEqualsLatchOrQuorum`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L98)
- [x] opinions, flags and reports equal a model of the spec — [`invariant_registryMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L111)
- [x] the frontier and heartbeats equal the model — [`invariant_frontierMatchesModel`](test/invariant/GuardianSignals.invariant.t.sol#L124)
- [x] unlock time is start plus base delay plus strike and suspect surcharges — [`invariant_unlockEqualsStartPlusBaseDelayPlusSignalSurcharge`](test/invariant/GuardianActivation.invariant.t.sol#L105)

### Trailing anchor oracle — [`test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol)
Actions: source steps, moves, jumps, outages and restamps of a stamped source, warps to the window edges, open updates, keeper ticks, a source settling at its pending anchor, acceptances, parameter changes and suspensions; once through each adapter and once with the parameters sized at a 98% LLTV. Its inline config sets 256 runs of depth 128.
- [x] the stored state equals the model's after every call — [`invariant_storedStateMatchesModel`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L100)
- [x] the stored parameters always pass the validator — [`invariant_parametersValid`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L105)
- [x] the anchor is never zero nor set in the future — [`invariant_anchorNonZeroAndNotInFuture`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L121)
- [x] a pending anchor's price and time are set together, never in the future, outside the anchor's bounds — [`invariant_pendingAnchorConsistent`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L128)
- [x] `price()` reverts with the predicted error, or returns the current price times the scale within the bounds — [`invariant_priceMatchesRule`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L138)
- [x] an update never stops or changes a price returned just before it — [`invariant_updateKeepsReturnedPrice`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L153)
- [x] every anchor move is a drift inside the rates' reach toward a price within the bounds, or a waited re-anchor onto the nearer target or the line — [`invariant_anchorMovesOnlyByReachOrWaitedSteps`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L161)
- [x] no update sets the anchor under the floor unless it already sat under it — [`invariant_anchorNeverUnderTheFloor`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L167)
- [x] no update leaves the anchor further over `basePrice` than both `maxAnchorAboveBase` of it and its gap before — [`invariant_anchorNeverOverTheCeiling`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L173)
- [x] `basePrice` follows its rule, and the anchor sits over it by at most `maxAnchorAboveBase` or the gap a narrower one left — [`invariant_basePriceFollowsItsRule`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L180)
- [x] a parameter change never changes a returned price — [`invariant_parameterChangesKeepReturnedPrice`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L191)
- [x] a suspension never ends more than `MAX_PRICING_SUSPENSION` after the call that set it — [`invariant_suspensionWithinCap`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L196)
- [x] with sized parameters, every returned price covers a debt borrowed at LLTV against the highest since the last acceptance or parameter change, incentive included — [`invariant_returnedPriceCoversDebtAtTheHighest`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L368)
- [x] each path the invariants rest on is reachable: drift, record, hold, expiry, re-anchor, floor, ceiling, pinned update, acceptance, and a taken and a refused parameter set — [`test_seededPaths_areNonVacuous`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L208)
- [x] a stamped source's refusals are reachable: a window closing while the price is stale, and a stamp after the block — [`test_stampCases_areReachable`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L310)
- [x] the sized campaign checks returned prices and never sends a set the sizing rule refuses — [`test_seededSizing_isNonVacuous`](test/invariant/TrailingAnchorOracle.invariant.t.sol#L374)

## Simulation testing
One market for 30 days (`test_shortRun_*`, in `make test-local`) or 365 days (`test_longRun_*`, `make test-simulation`), on a fixed and an adaptive IRM; a model predicts every outcome, and books and queue are reconciled every 20 iterations and after the unwind. The tests below are defined in [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol) and run in every suite.

- [x] a WETH/wstETH-shaped market with staking yield and no bad debt — [`test/simulation/WethWstethSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/WethWstethSolvency.t.sol), [`test/simulation/WethWstethAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/WethWstethAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol)
- [x] the same market with deep cuts and one crash; keepers write off bad debt — [`test/simulation/WethWstethCrashSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/WethWstethCrashSolvency.t.sol), [`test/simulation/WethWstethCrashAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/WethWstethCrashAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol)
- [x] a USDC/USDY-shaped market at 6/18 decimals whose price only rises — [`test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdySolvency.t.sol), [`test/simulation/UsdcUsdyAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol`](test/simulation/UsdcUsdyAdaptiveSolvency.t.sol)
- [x] one share above the personal allowance reverts — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_personalAllowance_oneShareAboveReverts`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L216)
- [x] a cut below one collateral unit is unavailable — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_seizureFloor_subUnitCutIsUnavailable`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L311)
- [x] a repay that would cross the rate ceiling reverts — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_borrowShareRate_atCeilingReverts`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L265)
- [x] a repay while paused reverts — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_repayWhilePaused_reverts`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L378)
- [x] past failures replay without a write-off or share residue — [`test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol): [`test_regressionSeed_noWriteOff_repaymentUnwind`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L109), [`test_regressionSeed_shareResidue_forcedExitUnwind`](test/simulation/SolvencySimulation.sol#L124)
- [x] the activation model the runs compare with matches the spec — [`test/unit/ActivationModel.t.sol`](test/unit/ActivationModel.t.sol)

## Deploy-script testing
Solidity parameters and recorded addresses; no TOML input or shell provisioning. The deployment suites run with `make test-deployment`.

- [x] backbone role handover, fee recipient and exit-book pausers — [`test/unit/ProtocolDeployer.t.sol`](test/unit/ProtocolDeployer.t.sol)
- [x] unknown run, wrong chain and absent core fail; recorded mainnet stays market-free and Hoodi market IDs match — [`test/unit/RecordedDeployment.t.sol`](test/unit/RecordedDeployment.t.sol)
- [x] missing markets deploy over an existing protocol; inert markets activate while active markets retain their state — [`test/deployment/ProtocolPreparation.t.sol`](test/deployment/ProtocolPreparation.t.sol): [`test_missingMarket_deploysOverExistingProtocol`](test/deployment/ProtocolPreparation.t.sol#L56), [`test_mixedMarkets_prepareInertAndPreserveActiveState`](test/deployment/ProtocolPreparation.t.sol#L34)
- [x] multiple markets share dependencies without redeploying recorded contracts — [`test/deployment/ProtocolPreparation.t.sol`](test/deployment/ProtocolPreparation.t.sol): [`test_multipleMissingMarkets_useSamePreparationAndSharedResources`](test/deployment/ProtocolPreparation.t.sol#L66), [`testRevert_recordedMissingCodeDoesNotDeployReplacement`](test/deployment/ProtocolPreparation.t.sol#L79)
- [x] incomplete records and unseeded active markets fail without repair — [`test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol): [`testRevert_partialRecordDoesNotDeployReplacement`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol#L103), [`testRevert_unseededCreatedMarketIsNotRepaired`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol#L117)
- [x] inert curve and activation settings match reviewed parameters — [`test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol): [`testRevert_inertCurveDiffersFromReviewedParameters`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol#L140), [`testRevert_inertActivationDiffersFromReviewedParameters`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol#L151)
- [x] a reused price feed rejects a wrong implementation, proxy, admin, collateral or zero price — [`test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol): [`testRevert_reusedPriceFeedHasDifferentImplementation`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol#L162), [`testRevert_reusedPriceFeedHasWrongProxyCode`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol#L185), [`testRevert_reusedPriceFeedHasWrongAdmin`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol#L171), [`testRevert_reusedPriceFeedHasWrongCollateral`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol#L202), [`testRevert_reusedPriceFeedHasZeroPrice`](test/deployment/MarketPreparation.t.sol#L193)
- [x] a market over a trailing anchor oracle: a new oracle is the spec's adapter, seeded in its proxy's constructor and held by the Agent alone; the Centrifuge kind — [`test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol): [`test_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_handsTheOracleToTheAgent`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L61), [`test_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_deploysTheCentrifugeAdapter`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L94)
- [x] a zero spec value, a seed outside its range, an Agent deployer, other market tokens, the base proxy path and a corridor too wide for the LLTV stop the run — [`test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol): [`testRevert_constructor_zeroSpecValue`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L135), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_seedOutsideRange`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L119), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_deployerIsAgent`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L166), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_marketTokensAreNotTheSpecs`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L172), [`testRevert_deployMarket_refusesAnUninitializedOracle`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L192), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_corridorTooWideForTheMarketLltv`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L199)
- [x] a reused trailing anchor oracle still matches the spec: tokens, parameters, roles, proxy admin, source, bounds and suspension — [`test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol): [`test_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_reusesAMatchingOracle`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L211), [`testRevert_deployMarket_reusedOracleForOtherTokens`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L250), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_reusedOracleHasOtherParameters`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L219), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_reusedOracleHasAnExtraManager`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L235), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_reusedOracleHasAnotherProxyAdmin`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L262), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_reusedOracleReadsAnotherSource`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L276), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_reusedOracleIsOutsideItsBounds`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L294), [`testRevert_deployTrailingAnchorMarket_reusedOracleIsSuspended`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorMarket.t.sol#L307)
- [x] the trailing anchor checks: the adapter build, the kind names and the kinds' pinned values, each launch parameter and role, the sizing rule on the live parameters, the anchor on and one wei past each line — [`test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol): [`test_kindName_namesEachKind`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L64), [`test_implementation_buildsTheUsdyAdapter`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L78), [`test_implementation_buildsTheCentrifugeAdapter`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L90), [`test_implementation_buildsTheChainlinkAdapter`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L99), [`test_kind_valuesArePinned`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L71), [`testRevert_implementation_centrifugeCollateralIsNotTheShareToken`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L114), [`test_checks_takeTheLaunchState`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L124), [`testRevert_checkLaunch_parameterMismatch`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L130), [`testRevert_checkLaunch_extraRoleHolder`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L162), [`testRevert_checkLaunch_missingRoleHolder`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L176), [`testRevert_checkLaunch_otherRoleHolder`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L190), [`testRevert_checkLive_corridorTooWideForTheLltv`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L208), [`test_checkLive_readsTheLiveParameters`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L219), [`test_checkLive_takesALowerLltv`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L228), [`test_checkLive_anchorOnTheFloorAndOneWeiBelowIt`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L234), [`test_checkLive_anchorOnTheCeilingAndOneWeiAboveIt`](test/unit/TrailingAnchorPriceFeed.t.sol#L259)

## Fork testing
Flows use a fresh fixture by default, or a recorded Solidity description under `script/deployment/<run>/artifacts/` with `RUN=<run>`. `PREPARE=false` checks the recorded state without preparing markets. Optional suites skip when their recorded dependencies are absent; a skip does not certify that component.

- [x] the activation rules on the live controller — [`test/fork/BorrowerActivation.t.sol`](test/fork/BorrowerActivation.t.sol): [`testRevert_withdrawCollateral_partOfInactiveLot_InactiveCollateralNotCleared`](test/fork/BorrowerActivation.t.sol#L38), [`test_topUp_beforeMaturity_movesDeadlineToTheNewQuote`](test/fork/BorrowerActivation.t.sol#L64)
- [x] lend, stake, borrow, repay and exit in one run — [`test/fork/HappyPathRoundTrip.t.sol`](test/fork/HappyPathRoundTrip.t.sol): [`test_happyPath_lendBorrowRepayExit`](test/fork/HappyPathRoundTrip.t.sol#L43)
- [x] healthy position, solvent liquidation and socialized loss — [`test/fork/Liquidation.t.sol`](test/fork/Liquidation.t.sol): [`testRevert_liquidate_healthyPosition`](test/fork/Liquidation.t.sol#L30), [`test_liquidateBadDebt_underwater_fullSeizeSocializesResidual`](test/fork/Liquidation.t.sol#L57)

- [x] freeze via the dashboard, unfreeze via the facade — [`test/fork/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol`](test/fork/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol): [`test_freeze_viaSteward_thenUnfreeze_viaFacade_borrowWorksAgain`](test/fork/FreezeLifecycle.t.sol#L34); [`test/fork/RiskStewardDashboardScenario.t.sol`](test/fork/RiskStewardDashboardScenario.t.sol): [`test_dashboard_freezesRealController_facadeUnfreezes`](test/fork/RiskStewardDashboardScenario.t.sol#L179)

## Acceptance testing
One market of a deploy run, on a fork. Expected values come from `script/deployment/<run>/artifacts/`, selected by `ACCEPTANCE_RUN`. Reviewed motion choices come from `ACCEPTANCE_PAUSER_COMMITTEE` and `ACCEPTANCE_ENABLE_SETTLEMENT`; activated checks also require `ACCEPTANCE_STEWARD`, `ACCEPTANCE_FEE`, `ACCEPTANCE_MIN_CORE_SEED_SHARES` and `ACCEPTANCE_MIN_VAULT_SEED_SHARES`. `make acceptance-run RUN=<run> MARKET=<market>`.

1. Before the activation motion — [`test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol); skips once the market is activated
   - [x] bytecode, wiring, config and exact role holders match the artifacts; the market is unbound and its accounting zero — [`test_deployedInertAndReadyForMotion`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L22)
   - [x] the exit-book pauser can pause both books — [`test_exitBookPauserPausesBothBooks`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L37)
   - [x] rejects a paused or frozen controller — [`testRevert_pausedControllerIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L41), [`testRevert_frozenControllerIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L67)
   - [x] rejects a wrong core owner or wrong bytecode — [`testRevert_wrongCoreOwnerIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L48), [`testRevert_wrongFacadeBytecodeIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L55), [`testRevert_wrongCircuitBreakerBytecodeIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L61)
   - [x] rejects an extra instant-activation caller or market steward — [`testRevert_unexpectedInstantActivationCallerIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L76), [`testRevert_unexpectedMarketStewardIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L86)
   - [x] rejects nonzero vault supply, a nonempty settlement queue or a wrong vault symbol — [`testRevert_nonZeroVaultSupplyIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L95), [`testRevert_nonZeroSettlementQueueIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L101), [`testRevert_wrongVaultSymbolIsRejected`](test/acceptance/MarketMotionReadiness.t.sol#L107)
2. After the motion — [`test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol`](test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol); skips before activation
   - [x] the market is created, bound and routed, with exact role holders — [`test_fullyConnectedConfiguredAndLive`](test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol#L20)
   - [x] the registered CircuitBreaker pauser pauses the controller and the registration clears — [`test_circuitBreakerPausesControllerAndClearsRegistration`](test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol#L33)
   - [x] the exit-book pauser pauses both books — [`test_exitBookPauserPausesBothBooks`](test/acceptance/MarketActivated.t.sol#L37)
3. A second activation — [`test/acceptance/MultiMarketActivationSimulation.t.sol`](test/acceptance/MultiMarketActivationSimulation.t.sol) (`make acceptance-run-multi`)
   - [x] activating market B leaves market A's routing, controller, pauser and allowlists intact — [`test_twoMarketsActivateWithoutCrossMarketStateCollision`](test/acceptance/MultiMarketActivationSimulation.t.sol#L8)
4. Fresh-fixture acceptance — [`test/fork/FreshDeploymentAcceptance.t.sol`](test/fork/FreshDeploymentAcceptance.t.sol)
   - [x] independent steward and fee expectations reject a mismatch — [`testRevert_wrongReviewedSteward_isRejected`](test/fork/FreshDeploymentAcceptance.t.sol#L56), [`testRevert_wrongFee_isRejected`](test/fork/FreshDeploymentAcceptance.t.sol#L69)
   - [x] missing core seed and insufficient vault seed fail; the exact seed floor passes — [`testRevert_missingCorePreseed_isRejected`](test/fork/FreshDeploymentAcceptance.t.sol#L76), [`testRevert_insufficientVaultPreseed_isRejected`](test/fork/FreshDeploymentAcceptance.t.sol#L86), [`test_seedThresholdBoundary_isAccepted`](test/fork/FreshDeploymentAcceptance.t.sol#L94)
   - [x] a second market is checked independently before and after activation — [`test_secondMarketIsReadyIndependently`](test/fork/FreshDeploymentAcceptance.t.sol#L27), [`test_secondMarketIsActivatedIndependently`](test/fork/FreshDeploymentAcceptance.t.sol#L99)
5. A market over a trailing anchor oracle, run locally — [`test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol)
   - [x] the oracle check and the role audit take the market that the recipe deploys — [`test_assertOracle_takesTheRecipesOracle`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol#L61), [`test_assertRoleHolderCompleteness_takesTheRecipesMarket`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol#L65)
   - [x] rejects a kind name of the other adapter, a record with two kinds, a record over another source and one for other tokens — [`testRevert_assertOracle_kindNameMismatch`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol#L70), [`testRevert_assertOracle_ambiguousRecord`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol#L77), [`testRevert_assertOracle_recordOverAnotherSource`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol#L84), [`testRevert_assertOracle_recordForOtherTokens`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol#L101)
   - [x] rejects a second manager, an LLTV the corridor does not fit and a second price acceptor — [`testRevert_assertOracle_extraManager`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol#L115), [`testRevert_assertOracle_corridorTooWideForTheMarketLltv`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol#L126), [`testRevert_assertRoleHolderCompleteness_extraPriceAcceptor`](test/unit/AcceptanceNavOracle.t.sol#L137)
6. Stages not covered by the recorded-deployment acceptance suites
   - [ ] a deployment with the backbone but no market (the suite needs `MARKET`)
   - [ ] a live market whose exit books do not allow it yet
   - [ ] a market after a real CircuitBreaker pause consumed the registration
   - [ ] role changes after launch ([`script/DEPLOYMENT.md`](script/DEPLOYMENT.md))

## state-mate
On-chain state against manually reviewed YAML. Maintain protocol and per-market configs under `deployed/<run>/state-mate/`; historical Hoodi checks remain under [`script/config/hoodi-testnet/state-mate/`](script/config/hoodi-testnet/state-mate/). State Mate checks one with `yarn start <config>`. Each config must describe the selected activation state; it is not generated from live state.

- [x] Hoodi, right after deploy: roles, parameters, proxies; markets inert — [`script/config/hoodi-testnet/state-mate/post-deploy.yaml`](script/config/hoodi-testnet/state-mate/post-deploy.yaml)
- [x] Hoodi, after the activation replay on a fork — [`script/config/hoodi-testnet/state-mate/post-activation-fork.yaml`](script/config/hoodi-testnet/state-mate/post-activation-fork.yaml)
- [x] Hoodi, live after the pending motions — [`script/config/hoodi-testnet/state-mate/live.yaml`](script/config/hoodi-testnet/state-mate/live.yaml)
- [x] mainnet protocol, factories, bundler/adapters and participating committee Safes — [mainnet protocol config](deployed/mainnet/state-mate/mainnet.yaml)
- [ ] post-deployment mainnet market and its IRM config: fill addresses and activation state in a separate market config after deployment

## Unreachable branches
Coverage misses two `src/` branches, on forge 1.7.1 and 1.8.3 alike.

- [-] `UnknownHookEvent` ([`src/controllers/LidoLendMarketController.sol:183`](src/controllers/LidoLendMarketController.sol#L183)): the ABI decoder rejects an out-of-range `HookEvent` first — [`test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol): [`test_on_outOfRangeHookEvent_isRejectedAtDecode`](test/scenario/SettlementSafety.t.sol#L688)
- [-] `price == 0` in the dust check ([`src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol:278`](src/libs/SettlementQueueLib.sol#L278)): its only caller returns earlier on a zero collateral value (line 163)
- [x] `quorum == 0` in the frontier sync ([`src/GuardianSignals.sol:395`](src/GuardianSignals.sol#L395)) holds on an uninitialized registry; `n < quorum` cannot hold — [`test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol): [`test_frontier_uninitializedRegistry_syncIsNoOp`](test/unit/GuardianSignals.t.sol#L701)
